Conclusion: RestorePath能够重建组织学图像中的诊断结构，防止高置信度错误预测并降低错误率，为百万像素级病理图像修复提供了全局一致的解决方案。

Abstract: Although deep learning has advanced Whole Slide Image (WSI) Analysis, tissue artifacts like bubbles and folds often cause silent failures by concealing essential morphology. Current pathology image restoration methods are mostly restricted to small patches, struggling to maintain global structural coherence at a megapixel scale. We introduce RestorePath, a framework for globally consistent megapixel scale inpainting that reconstructs diagnostic structures in histological image to prevent incorrect high-confidence predictions and lower error rates. Our model utilizes a Latent Diffusion Model (LDM) conditioned on Pathology Foundation Model (PFM) embeddings, integrating Large Kernel Attention (LKA) to manage long-range dependencies during random patch diffusion. Enhanced by Distance-Weighted Interpolation (DWI) and an Adaptive Guidance Scale (AGS), RestorePath ensures structural consistency and fidelity by modulating information from surrounding patches. Evaluations across TCGA-BRCA, BACH, and Camelyon16 datasets for images ranging from 512 to 4608 pixels demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in maintaining histological consistency. RestorePath significantly improves downstream Computational Pathology (CP) tasks, outperforming both raw artifact images and the conventional Detect-and-Discard (D&D) approach. The code is available at https://github.com/PathfinderLab/RestorePath

</details>


### [140] [Positive Pair Geometry Matters: Optimal Transport for Contrastive Learning of Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24125)
*Akshit Nanda,Shahzad Ahmad,Ram Prasad Padhy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OTCLR框架，利用熵最优传输位移插值生成几何一致的正样本视图，改进对比自监督学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比自监督学习多通过独立采样的随机数据增强构造正样本对，这种方式可能改变语义内容，并忽略数据分布的内在几何结构，从而影响表征质量。

Method: OTCLR不直接对比两个随机增强视图，而是在原图与其增强变体之间，通过熵最优传输位移插值构造中间视图，作为更好保留图像结构、显式建模空间分布几何的正样本；同时引入Sinkhorn正则项，促使传输插值视图与端点图像保持一致，以促进平滑的表征学习。该方法可直接嵌入标准对比学习流程，无需修改编码器结构。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，相比传统基于数据增强的对比学习基线，该方法提升了表征质量与迁移学习性能。

Conclusion: 将最优传输几何引入正样本构造，可生成几何一致、结构保真的视图，从而有效改进对比自监督表征学习，且兼容现有框架。

Abstract: Contrastive self-supervised learning has achieved strong performance by learning representations from multiple augmented views of the same image. However, most existing methods construct positive pairs using independently sampled stochastic augmentations, which may alter semantic content and ignore the intrinsic geometry of the data distribution. In this work, we propose OTCLR, an optimal transport-aware framework for contrastive learning representations that generates geometry-consistent positive samples. Instead of directly contrasting two randomly augmented views, we construct intermediate views between the original image and its augmented variants through entropic optimal-transport displacement interpolation. These transport-interpolated samples serve as positive views that better preserve image structure while explicitly modeling spatial distributional geometry. To further promote smooth representation learning, we evaluate auxiliary Sinkhorn regularization terms that encourage transport-interpolated views to remain consistent with their endpoint images. The proposed method can be incorporated into standard contrastive learning pipelines without modifying the encoder architecture. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets show that our approach improves representation quality and transfer learning performance compared with conventional augmentation-based contrastive learning baselines.

</details>


### [141] [Action-Slot: Structured Action-Centric Representation Learning for Multi-Agent Atomic Activity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24127)
*Yu-Ho Chang,Chi-Hsi Kung,Yi-Hsuan Tsai,Yi-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Action-Slot框架，将原子交通活动理解重构为结构化动作分解，结合类别对齐动作槽、并行时空槽更新及背景正则化，实现多智能体多标签活动的识别与弱监督定位，并构建了TACO数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子活动理解需识别并定位编码运动模式及其在道路拓扑中落地的结构化交通行为，其特点是多智能体、多标签、拓扑感知，且多个活动共现而许多智能体处于非活跃状态；传统动作识别方法以及置换不变、面向物体级归纳偏置的slot attention均与原子活动语义不匹配。

Method: 将slot学习重构为结构化活动分解，包含三项设计：1）类别对齐的动作槽，将槽锚定到预定义活动类别；2）并行时空槽更新，实现整体视频级推理；3）背景与负槽正则化，促进前景活动与无关区域竞争。此外提出基于注意力差异的伪掩码选择框架，通过测量候选区域移除前后的注意力变化抑制误报，实现无需密集标注的弱监督定位。

Result: 在OATS、TACO以及标注的nuScenes上，实验显示该方法具有更优的识别性能、较强的仿真到真实迁移能力，并达到当前最优的弱监督定位效果。

Conclusion: Action-Slot通过动作中心归纳偏置从原始视频直接解耦并发与异步活动，学习到的表征编码可迁移的时空落地信号；同时提出的TACO合成数据集提供完整原子活动覆盖与像素级标注，支持系统评估。

Abstract: Atomic activity understanding aims to recognize and localize structured traffic behaviors that jointly encode motion patterns and their grounding in road topology. Unlike conventional action recognition, atomic activities are multi-agent, multi-label, and topology-aware: multiple activities co-occur while many agents remain inactive. We introduce Action-Slot, a structured action-centric representation learning framework. Slot attention is widely used for object-centric decomposition, but its permutation-invariant design and object-level inductive bias are misaligned with atomic activity semantics. We reformulate slot learning as structured activity decomposition through three designs: (1) category-aligned action slots that anchor slots to predefined activity categories, (2) parallel spatio-temporal slot updating for holistic video-level reasoning, and (3) background and negative-slot regularization that enforces competition between foreground activities and irrelevant regions. Together these establish an activity-centric inductive bias that disentangles concurrent and asynchronous activities directly from raw video. Beyond recognition, the learned representations encode transferable spatio-temporal grounding signals. We further propose an attention-difference-based pseudo mask selection framework that suppresses false positives by measuring attention changes before and after candidate region removal, enabling weakly supervised localization without dense annotations. To support systematic evaluation, we introduce TACO, a balanced synthetic dataset with full atomic activity coverage and pixel-level annotations. Experiments on OATS, TACO, and annotated nuScenes show superior recognition, strong sim-to-real transfer, and state-of-the-art weakly supervised localization.

</details>


### [142] [The Visual Target Matters: Learning across the Visual Hierarchy for Brain-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24136)
*Ye Wang,HaoKun Ren,Hong Yu,Ruirui Li,Xiao Li,Ke Liu,Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 NeuroGlyph，从冻结视觉骨干网络的多层深度中学习试验无关的视觉目标，并将其分解为因子特定子空间，每个子空间学习图像条件下的深度分配，融合后用于脑到图像检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑到图像检索通常让神经编码器去拟合固定的最终层视觉目标，把视觉层级压缩为单一指定端点，导致其他深度的表征无法直接影响检索目标。作者希望学习视觉层级中不同深度信息应如何贡献于检索目标。

Method: NeuroGlyph 从冻结视觉骨干网络的多个深度学习一个与试验无关的视觉目标，将目标分解为因子特定子空间；每个子空间学习图像条件下的视觉深度分配，最终将各子空间融合为单一嵌入用于检索。

Result: 在 THINGS-EEG 和 THINGS-MEG 上，NeuroGlyph 在所有受控比较中优于最终层监督，并在四项比较中的三项超过事后最佳固定层 oracle。参数匹配消融实验支持因子化目标构建和图像条件深度分配的必要性。在可比的 200 类检索协议下，NeuroGlyph 在八项报告指标中的六项取得最强系统级性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，应学习跨视觉层级的检索目标，而不是指定单一视觉深度；跨深度、图像条件下的因子化目标构建能有效提升脑到图像检索性能。

Abstract: Brain-to-image retrieval seeks to identify the visual stimulus that elicited a non-invasive neural response. Candidate images are typically represented by pretrained vision models, whose internal representations vary in abstraction across depth. Existing methods usually train the neural encoder to recover a fixed final-layer visual target. Under this formulation, the visual hierarchy is reduced to a single prescribed endpoint, preventing representations at other depths from directly shaping the visual target. This limitation motivates learning how information across visual depths should contribute to the retrieval target. To this end, we introduce NeuroGlyph, which learns a trial-independent visual target from multiple depths of a frozen visual backbone. NeuroGlyph decomposes the target into factor-specific subspaces. Each subspace learns an image-conditioned allocation over visual depth. The resulting subspaces are fused into a single embedding for retrieval. Across THINGS-EEG and THINGS-MEG, NeuroGlyph outperforms final-layer supervision in all controlled comparisons. It also surpasses the post hoc best fixed-layer oracle in three of four comparisons. Parameter-matched ablations support both factorized target construction and image-conditioned depth allocation. Under comparable 200-way retrieval protocols, NeuroGlyph achieves the strongest system-level performance in six of eight reported metrics. These results support learning retrieval targets across the visual hierarchy rather than prescribing one visual depth.

</details>


### [143] [STAR: Scene- and Task-Aware 4D Radar Preprocessing Towards End-to-End Cognitive Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24151)
*Seung-Hyun Song,Dong-Hee Paek,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出场景与任务感知的雷达预处理器STAR及端到端训练框架，让预处理直接面向下游感知任务生成任务相关点云，在K-Radar上达到74.3 AP，超越之前最优5.6 AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达对光照和恶劣天气鲁棒，是环境感知的关键传感器。但传统预处理（如CFAR）仅依据信号层面准则筛选测量，会在点云生成中丢弃对下游感知有价值的信息；同时现有4D雷达感知流程将数据处理与下游感知独立优化，任务目标无法指导预处理阶段。

Method: 提出场景与任务感知的雷达预处理器STAR Preprocessor，并结合端到端训练框架。STAR引入场景上下文和下游任务目标，生成任务相关的雷达点，使雷达表示可直接针对感知任务进行优化。同时将STAR生成的任务相关点应用于多种现有3D检测器进行验证。

Result: 在K-Radar基准上达到74.3 AP，超过此前最优方法5.6 AP；将STAR生成的任务相关点应用于多种现有3D检测器时，在多数评测设置下提升检测性能，且相对传统预处理的点云取得整体正向的平均增益。

Conclusion: 将场景与任务信息融入雷达预处理并采用端到端优化，可生成更有利于感知的点云表示，显著提升4D雷达3D检测性能，并具备跨检测器的泛化增益。

Abstract: Four-dimensional (4D) Radar has emerged as a key sensor for environmental perception, providing range, azimuth, elevation, and Doppler measurements while remaining robust to illumination changes and adverse weather conditions. However, conventional Radar preprocessing methods, such as constant false alarm rate (CFAR) detection, select measurements primarily based on signal-level criteria and may therefore discard information valuable for downstream perception during point cloud generation. In addition, existing 4D Radar perception pipelines typically optimize Radar data processing and downstream perception independently, preventing task objectives from directly guiding the preprocessing stage. To address these limitations, we propose a Scene- and Task-Aware Radar (STAR) Preprocessor together with an end-to-end training framework. The STAR Preprocessor incorporates scene context and downstream task objectives to generate task-relevant Radar points, enabling the Radar representation to be optimized directly for perception. On the K-Radar benchmark, the proposed method achieves 74.3 AP, outperforming the previous state of the art by 5.6 AP points. Furthermore, applying the task-relevant points generated by STAR to various existing 3D detectors improves detection performance in most evaluation settings and yields an overall positive average gain over point clouds produced by conventional preprocessing.

</details>


### [144] [Relightable 3D Avatar Reconstruction with Semantic-Adaptive Motion-Illumination Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24158)
*Jiankuo Zhao,Xiangyu Zhu,Jijie Li,Baiqin Wang,Shukai Chen,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAMIRA：面向单目视频的3D高斯头像框架，通过语义自适应运动与光照响应建模，提升细粒度表情重建与重光照真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频重建可表现、可重光照的3D头部头像仍具挑战，需同时建模非刚性面部运动与光照相关外观。现有高斯头像方法多采用全局耦合表示，让高斯共享统一的运动或光照响应模型，忽略了不同面部语义区域各自的运动模式与材质/反射特性，限制了动画精度与重光照合理性。

Method: 提出SAMIRA语义自适应运动-光照响应建模框架。运动侧：语义自适应运动响应模块将当前到参考的网格位移光栅化到拓扑一致的UV空间，利用面部语义将位移特征通过语义专属调制器路由，预测局部高斯几何残差，超越粗略网格绑定。光照侧：语义自适应光照响应模块为每个面部区域学习紧凑的漫反射与镜面反射响应因子，使不同区域高斯能适应新环境光照，并将这些因子融入延迟物理着色，实现语义相关光照效果的轻量近似。

Result: 在自驱动重演、跨身份重演与重光照任务上的大量实验表明，SAMIRA在细粒度表情重建和重光照真实感方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过按面部语义解耦运动与光照响应，可有效弥补全局耦合表示的不足，为可动画、可重光照的高斯头部头像提供更精细、更合理的建模方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing expressive and relightable 3D head avatars from monocular videos remains challenging in computer vision, as it requires accurate modeling of both non-rigid facial motion and illumination-dependent appearance. Existing Gaussian avatar methods commonly rely on globally coupled representations, in which Gaussian primitives share a unified motion or illumination response model. Such uniform modeling neglects the distinct motion patterns and material/reflectance properties of different facial semantic regions, thereby limiting fine-grained animation accuracy and reducing relighting plausibility. To address this limitation, we propose SAMIRA, a 3D Gaussian avatar framework for semantic-adaptive motion-illumination response modeling. For motion response modeling, the Semantic-Adaptive Motion Response module rasterizes current-to-reference mesh displacements into a topology-consistent UV space and leverages facial semantics to route displacement features through semantic-specific modulators, predicting localized Gaussian geometric residuals beyond coarse mesh binding. For illumination response modeling, the Semantic-Adaptive Illumination Response module learns compact diffuse and specular response factors for each facial region, allowing Gaussians in different regions to adapt their illumination responses to novel environment lighting. These response factors are incorporated into deferred physically based shading, providing a lightweight approximation of semantic-dependent illumination effects. Extensive experiments on self-reenactment, cross-reenactment, and relighting demonstrate that SAMIRA improves both fine-grained expression reconstruction and relighting realism over existing methods.

</details>


### [145] [An Unexpected Robot Policy: Early Evaluations of GPT-6 Astra on RoboDojo and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24170)
*Wenbo Zhang,Kaixuan Wang,Yutao Ouyang,Xiaoyu Huang,Liyang Li,Kailun Su,Weiyang Jin,Wenhao Chai,Haotian Liang,Zhiyang Dou,Yue Chen,Tianxing Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"LLM as policy"设定，检验大语言模型能否不经任务微调直接充当机器人操作策略。在RoboDojo全部42项任务上评测三个LLM并与40个公开策略对比，GPT-6 Astra以22.48%平均成功率、28.97分超越所有公开条目，但GPT-5.5与DeepSeek-Flash仅0.88%和1.92%。Astra呈现两极化的能力画像：擅长语义理解类任务，不擅长高精度控制、动态控制与复杂双手协调；单样本演示无整体收益，但部分交互轨迹显示回合内扰动纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能系统通常分为System 1（高频动作生成的预训练策略）与System 2（视觉语言模型的高层规划）。本文追问：能否让LLM本身直接充当操作策略，而无需针对具体任务微调，即"LLM as policy"这一设定。

Method: 在RoboDojo全部42项任务上评测三个LLM，并与40个公开策略的成绩对比。Astra与GPT-5.5采用官方每任务50回合协议，DeepSeek-Flash采用每任务10回合；三者使用相同的后处理。另开展上下文实验，考察单样本演示与交互轨迹的影响。

Result: GPT-6 Astra在2100次试验中取得22.48%平均成功率与28.97分，高于所有公开条目；而GPT-5.5与DeepSeek-Flash在相同后处理下平均成功率仅为0.88%与1.92%。Astra能力分化明显：对需要语义理解、不要求高精度控制的任务泛化良好，在精度、动态控制或复杂双手协调任务上表现较差。单样本演示无整体收益，部分交互轨迹显示回合内扰动纠正。

Conclusion: 所评测的LLM在操作性能上差异悬殊，Astra表现突出，为通用操作模型的潜力提供了初步证据，但可靠的精度与动态控制仍是该评测设定下的局限。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems are often organized into System 1 and System 2. System 1 is typically a pretrained policy that generates actions at high frequency, whereas System 2 is often instantiated as a vision-enabled language model for high-level planning. We ask whether a large language model (LLM) can act as the policy for robot manipulation without task-specific finetuning. We call this setting LLM as policy. We evaluate three LLMs on all 42 RoboDojo tasks and compare their scores with 40 public policies. Astra and GPT-5.5 use the official 50-episode-per-task protocol; DeepSeek-Flash uses 10 episodes per task. GPT-6 Astra achieves 22.48% average success rate and 28.97 Score over 2,100 trials, ranking above every public entry. Yet GPT-5.5 and DeepSeek-Flash reach only 0.88% and 1.92% average success rate with the same post-processing. We find that Astra exhibits a sharply polarized capability profile. It generalizes well to tasks that require semantic understanding but not high-precision control. In contrast, it performs poorly on tasks that require precision, dynamic control, or complex bimanual coordination. In-context experiments show no aggregate benefit from one-shot demonstrations, while selected interaction traces show within-episode corrections under perturbations. Overall, the evaluated LLMs vary substantially in manipulation performance. Astra stands out and provides initial evidence for the potential of a general-purpose manipulation model, although reliable precision and dynamic control remain limitations in the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [146] [LegendBench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Legend Understanding with Counterfactual Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24172)
*Xinnuo Zhang,Zhike Tang,Jing Xu,Haoyuan Zhao,Weikai Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LegendBench 基准与生成流程，围绕图例理解构建细粒度诊断与受控测试，揭示 VLM 在图例-标记绑定与反事实一致性上的瓶颈，并证明针对性微调可有效弥补局部能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图例是图表理解的基础，可靠解读需要正确地将图例条目与对应视觉标记绑定。然而现有视觉语言模型（VLM）的图表理解评估依赖聚合准确率，易被表层捷径满足，且将图例相关错误与其他推理失败混在一起，无法定位具体缺陷。因此需要细粒度、可控的图例专项评测手段。

Method: 提出 LegendBench：一个参数化基准与生成流程，产出以图例为中心的定向测试样例。包含两部分：(1) 能力-任务分类体系，覆盖图例解析、图例定位、图例条件推理、图例感知拒答，用于定位失败环节；(2) 反事实组生成，每张基础图表在受控图例干预下生成多个变体，以探测模型的不变性与敏感性。利用该基准评估通用 VLM 与专用图表模型，生成能力画像，并据此指导针对性微调。

Result: 通过 LegendBench 评估通用 VLM 与专用图表模型并生成能力画像，发现其在可靠的图例到标记绑定和反事实一致性方面存在持续性瓶颈。基于能力画像的针对性微调可有效弥补局部能力缺口，并泛化到未见数据。此外，借助反事实设计开展细粒度诊断实验，分析了编码通道效应、图例顺序捷径以及不同可见性下的拒答行为。

Conclusion: LegendBench 提供了可定位、可控的图例理解评测框架，弥补聚合准确率诊断不足的问题；能力画像指导的瓶颈特异性干预能有效闭合局部能力缺口，反事实设计也支持深入的诊断性分析，为图表理解模型的评估与改进提供了新工具与思路。

Abstract: Legends are fundamental to chart understanding, as reliable interpretation requires correctly binding legend entries to corresponding visual marks. While vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to chart understanding, their legend understanding is poorly diagnosed by aggregate accuracy, which can be satisfied by superficial shortcuts and confound legend-specific errors with other reasoning failures. To enable fine-grained diagnosis and controlled testing, we introduce LegendBench, a parametric benchmark and generation pipeline that produces targeted legend-centric test cases. LegendBench contributes (1) a capability-task taxonomy spanning legend parsing, legend grounding, legend-conditioned reasoning, and legend-aware abstention to localize failures, and (2) counterfactual group generation, where each base chart yields multiple variants under controlled legend interventions to probe model invariance and sensitivity. Using LegendBench, we evaluate both general-purpose VLMs and specialized chart models and generate their capability profiles, revealing persistent bottlenecks in reliable legend-to-mark binding and counterfactual consistency. We then use these capability profiles to guide targeted fine-tuning, demonstrating that bottleneck-specific interventions can effectively close the localized capability gaps and generalize to unseen data. We further leverage our counterfactual design to conduct fine-grained diagnostic experiments, analyzing encoding-channel effects, legend-order shortcuts, and abstention under varying visibility.

</details>


### [147] [Benchmarking Off-the-Shelf Multimodal AI Models Against Dermatologists on Patient-Captured Skin Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24190)
*Rian Dolphin,Laura Knowles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估三款中低价位多模态前沿AI模型在皮肤科诊断任务中的表现，并与三名认证皮肤科医生对比，得出四项发现：AI一致性接近或略低于人类、置信度校准差、患者元数据影响因模型而异、模型成本不预示性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来AI快速发展，多模态能力（尤其视觉能力）成为前沿模型的核心特性。作者希望评估当前可负担的AI模型在皮肤科图像诊断上的实际能力下限，并检验其在临床场景中的可靠性，包括与人类专家的对比、置信度可用性、元数据影响及成本与性能的关系。

Method: 选取三款近期发布、定价处于低至中档的AI模型，针对患者提交图像进行皮肤病诊断评估；以三名认证皮肤科医生组成的评审小组作为对照，由医生对每张图像进行评分；比较AI与人类在临床医生间一致性指标上的表现，并考察AI置信度评分、附加患者元数据以及模型成本对性能的影响。

Result: 结果显示：1）按不同指标，AI模型在临床医生间一致性上要么与人类持平，要么略低于人类；2）要求AI给出置信度评分会产生校准较差的结果，临床环境中不应依赖置信度阈值；3）提供额外患者元数据的影响具有强模型特异性，三款模型中有一款在所有指标上均出现退化；4）模型成本并不能预测性能，表现最好的模型平均每例成本为0.0045美元。

Conclusion: 当前低至中档价位的多模态AI模型在皮肤科图像诊断上已接近人类专家的一致性水平，但仍存在置信度校准不足等问题，且元数据增益和成本效益因模型而异。临床使用时需谨慎对待AI置信度，并重视模型选择与验证，而非仅依据价格判断能力。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) has advanced at a rapid pace in recent years. Initially, breakthroughs in large language models caught widespread attention. However, recent generations of frontier AI models have adopted multimodal capabilities as a first class citizen, with vision capabilities being central to that. In this paper, we evaluate three recently released models on the task of diagnosing dermatological conditions from patient-submitted images. The models chosen are at the low to mid tier in terms of pricing and thus represent a floor on current AI capabilities, not a ceiling. We evaluate AI performance relative to a panel of three certified dermatologists, who grade each image, and we present four interesting findings. Firstly, depending on the metric, the tested AI models are either on par or slightly trail humans in terms of inter-clinician agreement. Secondly, we find that asking AI models for a confidence rating produces poorly calibrated answers, meaning use of confidence thresholds should not be relied upon in a clinical setting. Thirdly, the effect of providing additional patient metadata is strongly model-specific, with one of the three models degrading on every metric considered. Finally, model cost is not predictive of performance. The best-performing model we tested costs on average $0.0045 per case.

</details>


### [148] [Lightweight Pedestrian Head-Orientation Recognition Network for Safe Pedestrian-Vehicle Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24193)
*Yuanzhe Li,Yidi Huang,Xiaotong Chang,Hounian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级 LRHO-CNN 用于低分辨率行人头部朝向识别，并构建八类朝向新数据集；与 ResNet-18/34、VGG-16 对比精度最高，且在 JAAD、PIE 上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人头部朝向可为自动驾驶提供注意力与过街意图线索，但真实交通场景中头部区域分辨率低，可靠识别困难。

Method: 从多个公开数据集提取行人头部图像，人工标注为八类朝向，并进行系统预处理与数据增强；设计轻量级低分辨率头部朝向卷积网络 LRHO-CNN，与微调的 ResNet-18、ResNet-34、VGG-16 进行对比实验。

Result: LRHO-CNN 在对比模型中取得最高分类精度；在 JAAD 和 PIE 数据集上进一步评估，证明其在真实交通场景中识别头部朝向的有效性，并能提供支持下游行人行为与意图预测的朝向线索。

Conclusion: LRHO-CNN 是一种适用于低分辨率真实交通场景的轻量级行人头部朝向识别方案，可为自动驾驶中的行人行为与意图预测提供有用线索。

Abstract: Pedestrian head orientation recognition plays an important role in autonomous driving by providing valuable cues for understanding pedestrian attention and anticipating potential crossing behavior. However, reliable recognition in real-world traffic scenes remains challenging because pedestrian head regions are often captured at low resolution. To address this challenge, we propose a lightweight Low-Resolution Head Orientation Convolutional Neural Network (LRHO-CNN) for pedestrian head orientation recognition. We construct a new dataset by extracting pedestrian head images from multiple public datasets and manually annotating them into eight orientation categories. The collected images are systematically preprocessed and augmented to increase data diversity and better represent variations in illumination and image quality. The experimental analysis compares LRHO-CNN with three fine-tuned baseline models, namely ResNet-18, ResNet-34, and VGG-16. The results demonstrate that LRHO-CNN achieves the highest classification accuracy among the evaluated models. LRHO-CNN is further evaluated on the JAAD and PIE datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness in recognizing pedestrian head orientation in real-world traffic scenes and providing informative head-orientation cues that can support downstream pedestrian behavior and intention prediction.

</details>


### [149] [SAFe: Segment-guided Aggregation of Feature Densities for Anomaly-aware Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24204)
*Anja Delić,Jurica Runtas,Marin Oršić,Ivan Marković,Ivan Petrović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SAFe，一种基于类条件密度估计的生成式方法，用于视觉分割中的分布外（异常）目标检测，并在多个基准上取得新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中的视觉分割系统会遇到训练分布之外的目标，影响依赖场景解析的可靠自主系统。现有基于自监督基础模型的密度估计方法存在特征语义差或缺乏空间一致性等问题，损害下游决策。

Method: SAFe在冻结的DINOv3特征上训练轻量级归一化流，进行类条件归一化似然估计；结合transformer特征的密度估计与多尺度卷积特征的密度分数，兼顾全局语义和局部细节。此外引入基于SAM3的后处理，将逐位置似然连接为空间连贯的分割区域、抑制误检，并支持无需重训练的实例级异常检测，同时通过基于相似度的凝聚聚类区分异常目标中的新类别。

Result: SAFe在PANIC、OoDIS、SMIYC ObstacleTrack上取得新的最优性能，并在ISSU基准上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 通过类条件密度估计结合多尺度特征与基于SAM3的空间一致后处理，SAFe有效提升了分布外目标分割的语义质量与空间一致性，并可扩展至实例级异常检测和新类别区分。

Abstract: Visual segmentation systems encounter objects outside their training distribution during real-world deployment, hindering reliable autonomous systems that depend on scene parsing in the perception stage. Many recent methods address this by using self-supervised foundation models to train density estimators that yield low likelihood in anomalous image regions. Although promising, these methods suffer from poor feature semantics or they lack spatial consistency, both of which undermine critical downstream decisions. We address this problem with~\method, a generative method based on class-conditional density estimation over self-supervised representations. SAFe trains lightweight normalizing flows that produce class-conditional normalized likelihood estimates over frozen DINOv3 features. We combine density estimates from transformer features with density scores over multi-scale convolutional features to capture both global semantics and local detail. We introduce a method-agnostic post-processing step based on SAM3 that connects per-location likelihoods into spatially coherent segments while suppressing false positives, and enables instance-level anomaly detection without retraining. The post processing further distinguishes novel categories among anomalous objects by a similarity-based agglomerative clustering scheme. SAFe sets a new state of the art on the PANIC, OoDIS, SMIYC ObstacleTrack with strong performance on the ISSU benchmark.

</details>


### [150] [CoaG: Cylinders on a Grid: Coarse 3D Layout Control for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24208)
*Zhangsihao Yang,Mengyi Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 仅需地面网格与每人一个圆柱体的极简几何输入，即可同时控制生成视频中人物的站位与相机运动；作者自建2000条几何-视频配对数据，训练LoRA实现照片级真实感可控视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让用户以尽可能少的几何绘制（低门槛）同时控制生成视频中人物布局与相机轨迹，解决现有方法在人与相机联合控制上依赖复杂输入或缺乏配对数据的问题。

Method: 输入为地面网格、每人一个圆柱及相机路径（81帧）；外观由文本提示与背景参考图提供，布局与运动由几何提供。由于缺少现成配对数据，作者构建自动数据引擎：由组合种子生成2000条caption，用文本到视频模型各生成一段clip，再通过人物跟踪、背景修复、agentic地面掩码循环、前馈多视角重建与平面拟合将视频反向"提升"回几何，无需真实素材与人工标注。最终在Wan2.2-Fun-Control上训练LoRA，使用1935条样本。

Result: 在留出视频上，生成人物与圆柱的数量、顺序、位置、高度匹配；文本控制人物身份、参考图控制所处场景；dolly-in、orbit、pan、crane路径均能被较好跟随，仅dolly-out较弱。

Conclusion: 极简几何（地面平面+每人一圆柱）足以作为视频生成中人物与相机联合控制的统一信号，配合自动构造的大规模几何-视频配对数据与LoRA微调，可实现照片级真实感且高度可控的视频合成。

Abstract: We ask how little geometry a person has to draw to control both where people stand and where the camera moves in a generated video. Our answer is a ground plane and one cylinder per person. A user draws a grid on the ground, places one cylinder where each person should stand, moves the cylinders and the camera over 81 frames, and the model renders a photoreal video in which the people occupy the cylinders' positions, move as the cylinders move, and are seen from the drawn camera. Appearance comes from a text prompt and a background reference image; layout and motion come from the geometry. Because no dataset pairs such a signal with video, we build the pairs ourselves: an automatic engine writes 2000 captions from a combinatorial seed, generates a clip for each with a text-to-video model, and lifts every clip back to its geometry with person tracking, background inpainting, an agentic ground-mask loop, feed-forward multi-view reconstruction and a plane fit, with no real footage and no manual labels. A LoRA on Wan2.2-Fun-Control trained on 1935 such tuples follows drawn layouts and camera paths on hold-out clips: the generated people match the cylinders' count, order, position and height, the text changes who they are, the reference image changes where they are, and dolly-in, orbit, pan and crane paths are followed, dolly-out only weakly.

</details>


### [151] [ChartJudgeBench: Evaluating LMM Judges for Chart-to-Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24210)
*Lijian Wu,Henry Hengyuan Zhao,Zijian Zhang,Jiahao Tang,Jiajun Wu,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChartJudgeBench，一个用于评估图转代码任务中 LMM 评判者可靠性的视觉-语言基准，揭示了现有模型的四类系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表转代码系统日益依赖强化学习，而奖励信号质量至关重要。大型多模态模型常被用作视觉评论者和奖励模型，但其作为评判者的可靠性尚未被系统探究。

Method: 构建 ChartJudgeBench 诊断基准，包含 1,003 个图表感知对齐（CPA）成对比较实例，以及 650 个图表推理判断（CRJ）二分类接受/拒绝对比实例，覆盖图表复现与图表编辑任务，模拟智能体迭代优化与基于 RL 的图表优化中的核心评判决策。

Result: 对强 LMM 的评估揭示四类系统性局限：(i) 成对比较中的位置偏差；(ii) 过度预测"接受"的倾向；(iii) 难以匹配视觉风格与美感；(iv) RL 训练模型出现意外的宽松偏差。

Conclusion: 当前 LMM 评判者在作为图表转代码优化的评论者或奖励模型前，需要经过显式的可靠性验证。

Abstract: Building strong chart-to-code systems increasingly relies on reinforcement learning, whose effectiveness depends critically on the quality of the reward signal. Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) play a natural critical role in jointly assessing chart visual appearance and task requirements. They are therefore increasingly used as visual critics and reward models, yet their reliability as judges remains largely unexplored. To this end, we introduce ChartJudgeBench, a diagnostic vision-language benchmark for assessing LMM judges in chart-to-code workflows. It includes 1,003 Chart Perception Alignment (CPA) instances for pairwise chart comparison and 650 Chart Reasoning Judgment (CRJ) instances for binary Accept/Reject verification in Chart Reproduction and Chart Editing. Together, these tasks emulate the core judging decisions required in agentic refinement and RL-based chart optimization. Our evaluation of strong LMMs reveals four systematic limitations: (i) positional bias in pairwise comparison, (ii) a strong tendency to overpredict Accept, (iii) difficulty in matching visual styles and aesthetics, and (iv) an unexpected leniency bias in RL-trained models. These findings show that current LMM judges require explicit reliability validation before being used as critics or reward models in chart-to-code optimization. The code and data are available on ChartJudgeBench.

</details>


### [152] [Beyond Emotion Prompts: Fine-Grained Text-to-Image Generation Driven by Valence-Arousal-Dominance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24215)
*Minglang Li,Yueyue Fang,Xieping Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EMOTRANS，将心理学中的效价-唤醒-支配（VAD）坐标转化为图像生成条件，实现对情感风格的细粒度、可调控制，并构建了EMOVAD艺术绘画数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型虽能准确描绘主体与场景，但创作者难以在不重写内容描述的情况下指定图像应传达的细粒度情感。自然语言虽能暗示情感，却缺乏具有稳定含义和有序强度的控制尺度。

Method: 提出EMOTRANS，把基于心理学的VAD坐标转化为独立于内容文本的生成条件，并在不同去噪阶段进行调制；构建EMOVAD艺术绘画数据集，将客观内容描述与多位标注者单独收集的情感评分配对；通过共享模型的双分支训练协调情感表达与内容保持。

Result: 客观评估与人工评估表明，该框架提升了三维情感控制的准确性，产生了可感知、可排序的连续变化，同时保持了有竞争力的文本对齐度和图像质量。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种实用的情感驱动图像生成方法，将客观内容描绘扩展至细粒度情感调节，使情感风格成为可精细调整的创作变量。

Abstract: Although text-to-image models can accurately depict subjects and scenes, creators still struggle to specify the fine-grained emotions an image should convey without rewriting its content description. Natural language can suggest emotions, but it offers no control scale with stable meanings and ordered intensities. We propose EMOTRANS, which transforms psychologically grounded valence-arousal-dominance (VAD) coordinates into generation conditions that are independent of the content text and modulated across denoising stages, making emotional style a finely adjustable creative variable. To support this goal, we construct EMOVAD, an art-painting dataset that pairs objective content descriptions with separately collected emotional ratings from multiple annotators. We also coordinate emotional expression and content preservation through dual-branch training with a shared model. Objective and human evaluations show that the framework improves the accuracy of three-dimensional emotion control and produces perceptible, orderable continuous changes while maintaining competitive text alignment and image quality. This work provides a practical emotion-driven approach to image generation that extends objective content depiction to fine-grained emotional adjustment.

</details>


### [153] [Document Retrieval-Aware Chunking (D-RAC): Universal Retrieval-Aware Ingestion of Enterprise Documents via PDF Normalization and Multimodal Markdown Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24220)
*Uday Allu,Abhivanth Sivaprakash,Pratik Singh,Aman Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: D-RAC 是一种面向企业知识库的检索感知切块框架，通过将任意格式文档统一归一化为 PDF，再用多模态大模型单次转换为检索优化的 Markdown，并复用 W-RAC 的确定性解析与标识符级切块规划，大幅降低切块成本与耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业知识库中的文档格式异构（PDF、Word、演示文稿、扫描件），内容常被复杂视觉布局、多栏页面和密集表格封锁。基于规则的抽取和 OCR 会破坏阅读顺序、压平表格并丢失标题层级；而完全依赖智能体的切块方式则在抽取文本上产生高 token 成本与幻觉风险。因此需要一种既能处理任意可渲染格式、又保持低成本、确定性与可观测性的切块方案。

Method: D-RAC 将 W-RAC 框架扩展到任意文档格式：首先把任意输入文档归一化为 PDF（利用几乎每种格式都有忠实、确定性 PDF 渲染的特性）；随后用一次多模态大模型处理，把渲染页面转换为检索优化的 Markdown——将表格改写为自包含的散文式陈述并保留标题层级；之后切块流程与 W-RAC 完全一致：先确定性解析为可被 ID 寻址的单元，再在标识符而非文本上进行轻量级大模型切块规划。切块过程中源文本从不被重新生成。

Result: 在 RAG-Multi-Corpus 基准的 PDF 子集（236 份文档、795 页，覆盖五个企业领域）上，D-RAC 用 72 分钟、零错误地完成全语料转换与切块，产出 1,748 个可直接用于检索的块。相比使用前沿大模型的智能体切块，D-RAC 将切块阶段输出 token 减少 95.7%，切块成本降低 77.8%（GPT-4.1 定价）至 85.6%（Gemini 2.5 Pro 定价），切块时间减少 75%，并可线性扩展到 500 页以上的文档。

Conclusion: D-RAC 通过“归一化为 PDF + 单次多模态转换 + 标识符级确定性切块”的组合，保留了 W-RAC 在成本、确定性和可观测性上的优势，同时让所有可渲染格式成为一等输入，为异构企业文档的检索增强生成提供了高效、可扩展的切块方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems over enterprise knowledge bases must ingest heterogeneous document formats -- PDFs, Word documents, presentations, and scans -- whose content is locked inside complex visual layouts, multi-column pages, and dense tables. Rule-based extraction and OCR destroy reading order, flatten tables, and lose heading hierarchy, while fully agentic chunking over extracted text incurs high token costs and hallucination risk. We present Document Retrieval-Aware Chunking (D-RAC), an extension of our Web Retrieval-Aware Chunking (W-RAC) framework to arbitrary document formats. D-RAC first normalizes any input document into PDF, exploiting the fact that virtually every format has a faithful, deterministic PDF rendering. A single multimodal LLM pass then converts rendered pages into retrieval-optimized Markdown -- rewriting tables as self-contained prose statements and preserving heading hierarchy -- after which chunking proceeds exactly as in W-RAC: deterministic parsing into ID-addressable units followed by lightweight LLM-based chunk planning over identifiers rather than text. Source text is never regenerated during chunking, preserving W-RAC's cost, determinism, and observability benefits while unlocking every renderable format as a first-class input. On the 236-document, 795-page PDF subset of the RAG-Multi-Corpus benchmark spanning five enterprise domains, D-RAC converts and chunks the entire corpus in 72 minutes with zero errors, producing 1,748 retrieval-ready chunks. Compared to agentic chunking with frontier LLMs, D-RAC reduces chunking-stage output tokens by 95.7%, cutting chunking cost by 77.8% (GPT-4.1 pricing) to 85.6% (Gemini 2.5 Pro pricing) and chunking time by 75%. D-RAC scales linearly to documents of 500+ pages.

</details>


### [154] [DiaSeg: Diagonal Segment Extraction from DTW Paths for Interpretable Gait Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24223)
*Tresor Y. Koffi,Amel Hidouri,Corentin Legrand,Aurélie Bertaux*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiaSeg从DTW最优规整路径中提取对角线片段，用五个几何特征刻画时序局部对齐信息，实现无监督模式发现与临床步态诊断的可解释特征提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DTW仅保留距离值而丢弃最优规整路径，损失了与临床诊断最相关的局部对齐信息，且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 提取DTW路径中的对角线片段（带受控断点），用五个几何特征（有效长度、中断次数、代价变化、时间位置、路径上下文）描述每段，实现无需领域特征工程的无监督模式发现；在91名被试、六种临床状况（健康衰老、帕金森、亨廷顿、ALS、脑肿瘤、中风）上验证。

Result: 对角线片段形成与生物力学相位标注一致的无监督模式（轮廓系数0.33），健康与病理步态分离度ARI达0.986；片段对病理判别达69%（监督）与75%（患者级聚类），病理表现为片段长度的分布偏移；结合片段与周期级特征后分类提升至91.7%。

Conclusion: DiaSeg将DTW从黑箱距离转化为可解释的时间特征来源，虽周期级方法准确率更高（91%），但片段提供步态周期内的相位特异性可解释性，定位协调性断裂之处，服务神经退行性疾病评估。

Abstract: Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) is the dominant approach for measuring similarity between time series, yet standard practice discards the optimal warping path after computing a single distance value, losing local alignment information most relevant to clinical diagnosis. We introduce DiaSeg, a framework that extracts diagonal segments from DTW paths with controlled breaks, characterizing each segment by five geometric features (effective length, interruption count, cost variation, temporal position, and path context), and enabling unsupervised pattern discovery without domain-specific feature engineering. Validated on 91 subjects across six clinical conditions (healthy aging, Parkinson's, Huntington's, ALS, brain tumor, and stroke), three findings emerge. First, diagonal segments form consistent unsupervised patterns (silhouette 0.33) aligned with biomechanical phase annotations, with label-based validation confirming near-perfect separation of healthy and pathological gait (ARI up to 0.986). Second, segments discriminate pathology at 69% (supervised) and 75% (patient-level clustering), with pathology manifesting through distributional shifts in segment length; combining segment and cycle-level features further improves classification to 91.7%. Third, while cycle-based methods achieve higher accuracy (91%), diagonal segments provide phase-specific interpretability unavailable in global representations, localizing where coordination breaks down within the gait cycle. DiaSeg thus transforms DTW from a black-box distance into a source of interpretable temporal features for neurodegenerative disease assessment.

</details>


### [155] [SRPR-Net: Semantic and Relational Prompt Refinement for Automated SAM-based Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24226)
*Lufei Liu,Guojie Li,Suncheng Xiang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SRPR-Net，通过语义与关系提示的序列化精炼实现基于SAM的自动化实例分割，在多个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示驱动的基础模型（如SAM）泛化能力强，但自动提示存在语义引导不足和实例间关系建模缺失的问题，限制了其在实例分割中的表现。

Method: 设计语义关系提示精炼网络（SRPR-Net）：采用序列化提示精炼机制，先用视觉-语言语义丰富检测器几何信息，再引入同图像实例依赖关系，在SAM分割前实现上下文感知的框调整。

Result: 在多个标准基准上，SRPR-Net的分割性能相较现有最先进方法取得一致提升。

Conclusion: SRPR-Net验证了语义与实例关系提示精炼对自动SAM分割的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Instance segmentation is a fundamental computer vision task with diverse real-world applications. Recently, prompt-driven foundation models have shown promising generalization. However, automated prompting remains limited by insufficient semantic guidance and inter-instance modeling. To address this challenge, we propose a novel architecture, named Semantic Relational Prompt Refinement Network (SRPR-Net), for automated SAM-based instance segmentation. A sequential prompt refinement mechanism is introduced to enrich detector geometry with visual-language semantics and then incorporate same-image instance dependencies, enabling context-aware box adjustment before SAM segmentation. Experiments on multiple standard benchmarks demonstrate that SRPR-Net achieves consistent improvements in segmentation performance over existing state-of-the-art approaches. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/JeremyXSC/SRPR-Net.

</details>


### [156] [IMPLICIT-Bench: Measuring Implicit Bias in Text-to-Image Models under Neutral Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24228)
*Yue Dai,Ziyang Liu,Marc Cheong,Caren Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IMPLICIT-Bench基准，通过知识图谱构建中性、刻板印象与反刻板印象三元组提示，揭示文本生成图像模型在自然提示下的隐性偏见，并发现去偏见方法与语义保真度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型偏见评估多采用槽位模板（如"一张[职业]的照片"），仅能探测孤立、显式的人口统计属性，忽视了自然提示中更广泛的隐性偏见：当刻板印象相关属性未被明确指定时，模型仍默认产生刻板化输出，而这类失败模式在现有评估中几乎不可见。

Method: 设计IMPLICIT-Bench基准，核心是基于结构化知识图谱构建受控提示三元组——中性、刻板印象和反刻板印象变体，三者仅在单一偏见维度上不同而保持场景语义一致，从而实现对偏见效应的精确归因。基准涵盖11个偏见类别、共5,493条提示，并通过多模型一致性、CLIP验证和人工评估进行验证。随后利用该基准评估去偏见方法。

Result: 实验表明，当前最先进的T2I模型在中性提示下表现出系统性偏见，这是一类现有评估方法基本无法察觉的失败模式；同时，在评估去偏见方法时发现偏见降低与语义保真度之间存在根本性权衡。

Conclusion: IMPLICIT-Bench为测量自然提示下的隐性偏见提供了可控、可归因的评估工具，揭示了T2I模型被忽视的系统性偏见问题，并指出去偏见面临偏见削减与语义保真难以兼顾的核心挑战。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models are typically evaluated for bias using slot-based templates such as ``a photo of a [profession]''. Such templates probe only \emph{explicit} demographic attributes (e.g., gender, skin tone) in isolation. They overlook a broader \emph{implicit} bias that arises in natural prompts: when stereotype-relevant attributes are left unspecified, models still default to stereotypical outputs. We introduce IMPLICIT-Bench, a benchmark for measuring implicit bias in T2I models under such prompts. The key design is a structured-knowledge-graph (KG) construction of controlled prompt triplets: neutral, stereotype, and anti-stereotype variants that differ only along a single bias dimension while preserving scene semantics. This enables precise attribution of bias effects that template benchmarks cannot achieve. IMPLICIT-Bench comprises 5,493 prompts across 11 bias categories, validated through multi-model agreement, CLIP-based verification, and human evaluation. Using this benchmark, we show that state-of-the-art T2I models exhibit systematic bias under neutral prompts, a failure mode largely invisible to existing evaluations. We then use IMPLICIT-Bench to evaluate debiasing methods, uncovering a fundamental trade-off between bias reduction and semantic fidelity.

</details>


### [157] [Look Where It Counts: A Free, Label-Free Visual Evidence Signal for Fine-Grained Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24244)
*Santi Ram Tiwari,Nihal Naik,Devbrat Pandey,Nishant Sinha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 多模态大模型在细粒度视觉问答上的失败主要源于高分辨率图像被下采样、模型看不到证据，而非推理能力不足。作者提出"对比证据差"（同一输出在相关区域与无关区域条件下的逐token对数似然比），并发现该信号可用于免训练的区域选择，将细粒度准确率从70%提升到85%，且能与模型置信度互补地预测正确性；但将其转化为训练方法（SEG-Distill）在试验规模下未能超越基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有补救手段代价高昂：需要标注答案（SFT）、人工设计验证器（RLVR）或大型外部教师模型（on-policy蒸馏）。作者希望探究视觉证据本身能否免费提供监督信号，从而避免这些昂贵依赖。

Method: 形式化"对比证据差"——模型在问题相关区域与无关区域条件下对自身输出的逐token对数似然比；在Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-8B、Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B三个模型和V*Bench上评测。免训练方法为：用单视图、无标签准则，选择使模型答案分布最尖锐的候选裁剪区域。此外将同一信号转为门控自蒸馏训练法（SEG-Distill），测试三种门控设计。

Result: 免训练区域选择的目标定位能力是随机概率的4.4–5.1倍，推理时将细粒度准确率从70%提升到85%。对比证据差与模型自身置信度互补，二者结合预测正确性优于单独使用，AUC最高达0.99；在高置信子集内识别"自信但错误"的答案，AUC为0.97–1.00。所有效应集中在感知瓶颈类问题上，在全局上下文对照问题上消失。负面结果：SEG-Distill在试验规模下未超越基座模型，且更激进的门控会降低准确率。

Conclusion: 视觉证据信号是真实存在的，可用于免训练的推理期区域定位与可靠性预测，但如何将其有效转化为训练增益仍是未解决问题。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) fail at fine-grained visual questions less because they cannot reason than because they never see the evidence: high-resolution images are downsampled before encoding, so the model answers from linguistic priors. The standard remedies are expensive: annotated answers (SFT), hand-engineered verifiers (RLVR), or a large external teacher (on-policy distillation). We ask whether the visual evidence itself can supply the signal for free. We formalize the contrastive evidence gap, the per-token log-likelihood ratio that a model assigns to its own output when conditioned on a question-relevant region versus an irrelevant one, and study it across Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-8B, and Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B on V*Bench. Our main positive result is training-free: selecting the candidate crop under which the model's answer distribution is most peaked, using a single-view, label-free criterion, discovers the answer-bearing region with no bounding boxes, training, or labels. It localizes the target 4.4 to 5.1 times better than chance and raises fine-grained accuracy from 70 percent to 85 percent at inference. We further show that the gap is complementary to the model's own confidence. Combining them predicts correctness better than either alone, with AUC up to 0.99, and flags confidently wrong answers, with AUC ranging from 0.97 to 1.00 within the high-confidence subset. All effects concentrate on perception-bottleneck questions and vanish on a global-context control. Finally, we report an honest negative result: converting the same signal into a training method, gated self-distillation (SEG-Distill), does not outperform the base model at pilot scale across three gate designs, while more aggressive gating degrades accuracy. The signal is real, but converting it into training gains remains an open problem.

</details>


### [158] [Hierarchical Prompt Learning for Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24276)
*Andro Erdelez,Pascal Mettes,Behzad Bozorgtabar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向冻结双曲视觉语言模型的层级提示学习插件，用父类提示、父级监督、双曲蕴含正则与父反馈logit融合增强类提示学习器，实例化为HyPLO/CoHyPLO/MaHyPLO，在11数据集上提升基础到新类泛化与跨数据集迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲视觉语言模型天然适合表示层级结构，但其下游适配多依赖固定提示；现有提示学习方法把类别标签当作扁平集合，未利用可用的分类学结构。因此作者希望弥合这一空白。

Method: 给定固定的离线父类层级，在冻结双曲VLM上为类提示学习器增加一个独立的父类提示学习器，并引入父级监督、双曲蕴含正则化与父反馈logit融合；将该插件套用到CoOp、CoCoOp、MaPLe，得到HyPLO、CoHyPLO、MaHyPLO。

Result: 在标准11数据集基准上，所有变体均改善基础到新类泛化与跨数据集迁移，在域偏移下与各自提示学习基线相当；六项层级指标与嵌入分析显示预测更符合分类学，父类、类与图像嵌入在双曲空间中形成层级一致的组织。增益在新类需放入固定分类学时最大，在同级类细粒度混淆或仅图像分布偏移时最小。

Conclusion: 将分类学层级结构显式注入冻结双曲VLM的提示学习是有效的，可提升泛化与迁移并产生层级一致的表示；其收益依赖任务对层级结构的需求程度。

Abstract: Hyperbolic vision-language models (VLMs) represent image and text features in a geometry naturally suited to hierarchy, but their adaptation to downstream tasks has largely relied on fixed prompts. Existing prompt learning methods, meanwhile, treat class labels as a flat set and do not exploit available taxonomic structure. We address this gap with a hierarchical prompt learning plug-in for frozen hyperbolic VLMs. Given a fixed offline parent-class hierarchy, it augments a class prompt learner with a separate parent prompt learner, parent-level supervision, hyperbolic entailment regularization, and parent-feedback logit fusion. We instantiate the method with CoOp, CoCoOp and MaPLe, yielding HyPLO, CoHyPLO and MaHyPLO. Across the standard 11-dataset benchmark, all variants improve base-to-new generalization and cross-dataset transfer, and remain comparable to their prompt learning baselines under domain shift. Six hierarchical metrics and embedding analyses show that the method produces more taxonomically consistent predictions and induces a hierarchy-consistent organization of parent, class, and image embeddings in hyperbolic space. Its gains are largest when novel classes must be placed within a fixed taxonomy, and smallest for fine-grained confusions among sibling classes or shifts affecting only the image distribution.

</details>


### [159] [Classifier-Free Guidance in Flow Matching: Non-Autonomous Potentials, Overshoot, and Posterior-Mean Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24287)
*Jishen Peng,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将 Flow Matching 视为时变梯度流，从几何角度解释 CFG 导致的分布扭曲，并提出无需训练的 PMC-CFG 方法自适应控制引导强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) 能提升 Flow Matching 的条件生成质量，但强引导会扭曲生成分布、降低多样性，其内在机制尚缺乏清晰的几何解释，也缺少有效的控制手段。

Method: 将 Flow Matching 建模为时变梯度流，刻画 CFG 如何重塑其潜在势函数，从而解释强对齐伴随的均值位移与轨迹集中现象；据此提出 Posterior-Mean-Capped CFG (PMC-CFG)，一种基于模型隐含终端后验均值的逐样本引导控制方法，无需额外网络评估且免训练。

Result: 在合成数据和大规模图像生成基准上的实验表明，PMC-CFG 能抑制引导引起的分布扭曲与轨迹集中，改善对齐—多样性权衡，尤其在标称引导强度较大时收益显著。

Conclusion: 通过几何视角揭示 CFG 的作用机制，并据此实现的 PMC-CFG 可在不增加计算开销的前提下自适应地保留最强可行引导，兼顾对齐与多样性。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) improves conditional generation in Flow Matching, but strong guidance can distort the generated distribution and reduce diversity. We provide a geometric account of this behavior by viewing Flow Matching as a time-varying gradient flow and characterizing how CFG reshapes its underlying potential. This view explains how stronger alignment can be accompanied by mean displacement and trajectory concentration, and motivates controlling guidance through the model-implied terminal posterior mean. We therefore propose Posterior-Mean-Capped CFG (PMC-CFG), a training-free, per-sample method that adaptively retains the strongest feasible guidance without additional network evaluations. Experiments on synthetic and large-scale image-generation benchmarks show that PMC-CFG limits guidance-induced distortion and concentration while improving the alignment--diversity trade-off, with particularly strong benefits when nominal guidance is large.

</details>


### [160] [HappyWorld-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24308)
*Zhiqi Bai,Junai Cai,Yixin Chen,Jingrun Du,Tao Feng,Wei Gong,Siyuan Huang,Xiao Lin,Jiaheng Liu,Jun Luo,Yongzhe Lyu,Liya Ma,Zenan Meng,Lin Qu,Wenbo Su,Jiaming Wang,Qinghe Wang,Shaofei Wang,Yanghai Wang,Zequn Wang,Ziming Wang,Hu Wei,Jiangtao Wu,Ruiqi Wu,Jiaxin Xie,Yuchi Xu,Ze Xu,Chengting Yu,Liangyu Yuan,Gang Zeng,Yawen Zeng,Xingyao Zhang,Zizheng Zhang,Bo Zheng,Jiancheng Zhu,Song-Chun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HappyWorld-Bench，一个从生成构建到统一世界建模的分层能力框架（W1-W6），覆盖视频、空间、具身三大评测赛道，结合人机 A/B 的 Elo 评级与自动化行为指标，对 14 个视频世界模型、9 个空间系统、8 个具身候选模型进行统一评测，揭示各赛道仍存在可靠性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评测世界模型不仅要看生成世界的质量，还需考察其在探索、交互与修改下的连贯性与响应能力；现有评测多偏重视觉质量，缺乏对状态一致性与行为正确性的系统衡量。

Method: 设计分层能力框架（六大世界能力 W1-W6），实例化于视频、空间、具身三条独立评测赛道；构建含 1,138 条视频提示、300 个空间场景、254 个具身测试用例的基准；运行 HappyWorld-Arena 组织人工 A/B 对比以得出模型级 Elo 评分，并配套新设计的自动化指标捕捉行为正确性。

Result: 在三赛道统一评测 14 个视频世界模型、9 个空间系统与 8 个具身候选模型。结果显示均存在可靠性缺口：视频模型在长程推演与重访时一致性下降；空间模型放置准确率最高仅 70.14%、编辑执行率 73.33%；具身模型难以在多步动作中保持状态，也难以精确响应改变的动作条件与物理规则。

Conclusion: 评测世界模型不应只看视觉质量，还应关注状态一致性及其对动作与干预响应的正确性。

Abstract: Evaluating world models requires assessing both the quality of the worlds they generate and their consistency and responsiveness under exploration, interaction, and modification. We introduce HappyWorld-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark that evaluates whether generated worlds remain reliable as agents interact with them. Our design is built on a hierarchical capability framework of six world capabilities (W1-W6), from generative construction to unified world modeling, instantiated across three independent evaluation tracks: video world models, spatial world models, and embodied world models. HappyWorld-Bench comprises 1,138 video prompts, 300 spatial scenes, and 254 embodied test cases. Across all three tracks, we build and operate HappyWorld-Arena to organize human A/B comparisons and derive model-level Elo ratings, which complement newly designed automated metrics that capture behavioral correctness. We evaluate 14 video world models, 9 spatial systems, and 8 embodied candidates under this unified framework. Results reveal remaining reliability gaps across all three tracks: video models exhibit reduced consistency during extended rollouts and revisits, spatial models achieve at best 70.14% placement accuracy and 73.33% edit execution, and embodied models struggle to preserve state across multi-step actions and respond precisely to altered action conditions and physical rules. These findings highlight the need to evaluate world models not only by visual quality, but also by state consistency and the correctness of their responses to actions and interventions.

</details>


### [161] [AnalogDepth: Multi-view Geometry from FPV drones under Analog Video Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24312)
*André Amorim,Pedro F. Proença*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 AnalogDepth，一种参数高效的训练流程，通过融合 LoRA 的 DINOv2 骨干与师生知识蒸馏，将 Depth Anything 3 适配到模拟 FPV 图传噪声图像，并证明使用真实噪声库比高斯噪声增强更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟视频传输（VTX）因低延迟、轻量和低成本在 FPV 无人机中广泛使用，但其图像退化具有复杂的空间结构特征，与标准训练增强所用的数字噪声（如 AWGN）本质不同。这类噪声会严重降低当前最先进的前馈视觉几何基础模型 Depth Anything 3（DA3）的精度，因此需要针对性的适配方法。

Method: 作者提出 AnalogDepth：一种参数高效的训练流程，采用师生知识蒸馏，并在 DINOv2 骨干中注入低秩适配（LoRA）。不同于解析式合成噪声，他们从多样条件下的静态 FPV 录像中构建噪声库，并将真实噪声注入与 PSD 匹配的高斯合成噪声及 AWGN 基线进行对比。

Result: 在三个室内场景的六段真实 FPV 飞行序列上，使用该噪声库训练相比预训练 DA3 基线以及两种高斯噪声变体，均能持续降低逐帧深度 RMSE 和 3D 重建 Chamfer 距离。

Conclusion: 复现真实模拟传输噪声的空间结构对于有效适配至关重要，真实噪声注入优于解析合成的高斯噪声方案。

Abstract: Analog video transmission (VTX) remains widespread in FPV drones due to low latency, weight and low cost. However analog VTX suffers from complex spatially structured image degradation which differ fundamentally from digital image corruption (e.g. AWGN) used in standard training augmentation. This work shows that this type of noise severely degrades the accuracy of Depth Anything 3 (DA3), a state-of-the-art feed forward visual geometry foundation model.
  To address this gap, we present AnalogDepth, a parameter-efficient training pipeline that adapts DA3 to analog FPV imagery using student-teacher knowledge distillation with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) injected into the DINOv2 backbone. Rather than synthesizing noise analytically, we build a noise bank from static FPV recordings under diverse conditions and compare real-noise injection against PSD-matched Gaussian synthesis and AWGN as baselines. Experiments on six real FPV flight sequences across three indoor scenes show that training with our noise bank consistently reduces per-frame depth RMSE and 3D reconstruction Chamfer distance compared to the pretrained DA3 baseline and both Gaussian noise variants. These results demonstrate that replicating the spatial structure of real analog transmission noise is critical for effective adaptation.

</details>


### [162] [NeuIDO: Neural Intrinsic Dynamics Operator for Physics-Informed 4D World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24313)
*Jiajing Lin,Xin Zhang,Jianhua Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出NeuIDO，将世界建模表述为神经算子学习问题，通过两阶段训练从视觉观测中学习统一的固有动力学表示，实现零样本动力学推断与少样本适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息4D生成依赖人工设定的动力学假设，而非内化世界动力学，距离真正的世界模型仍有差距。

Method: 将世界建模视为神经算子学习问题，采用两阶段训练策略，学习从视觉观测分布到固有动力学分布的泛化映射，并支持零样本推断与少样本适配。

Result: 实验表明NeuIDO能将不同视觉观测背后的固有动力学统一为共享表示，并能快速推断新场景中的动力学。

Conclusion: NeuIDO推动物理信息4D生成向真正的世界模型迈进，实现可泛化的动力学建模。

Abstract: World models aim to capture environmental dynamics and predict future trajectories, showing growing potential for embodied intelligence. Physics-informed 4D generation integrates physical simulation to predict 3D object interactions, offering a promising pathway toward world models. However, this paradigm relies on manually imposed dynamical assumptions rather than internalizing world dynamics, and thus still leaves a gap toward a true world model. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuIDO, a novel world dynamics modeling framework that learns a unified intrinsic dynamics representation from visual observations, advancing physics-informed 4D generation toward a world model. Specifically, we formulate world modeling as a neural operator learning problem and introduce a two-stage training strategy to learn a generalizable mapping from the visual observation distribution to the intrinsic dynamics distribution. Building on this observation-dynamics mapping, NeuIDO enables zero-shot dynamics inference directly from videos and can be further aligned with complex real-world dynamics via few-shot adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NeuIDO effectively unifies the intrinsic dynamics underlying diverse visual observations into a shared representation and rapidly infers dynamics in novel scenes.

</details>


### [163] [AlignMorph: Tuning-Free Diffusion Image Morphing via Explicit Semantic Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24330)
*Wuyi Liu,Xu Han,Yuren Chen,Yige Mao,Zishuo Peng,Xianzhi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AlignMorph，一种免调优扩散框架，遵循"先传输后去噪"原则，通过显式解耦几何对齐与生成去噪，解决图像变形中大幅布局差异下的对齐失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的图像变形方法要么需要昂贵的逐对优化，要么依赖隐式空间对齐，在布局差异较大时容易失效。作者希望避免结构纠缠，显式解耦几何对齐与生成去噪。

Method: AlignMorph 为免调优扩散框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 全局语义传输，通过熵正则最优传输与可靠性感知的隐变量变形实现扩散兼容的语义对齐；(2) 坐标对齐生成，使用对称双阶段注意力交接，在去噪过程中保持一致的坐标空间。

Result: 无需任何调优，AlignMorph 在变形基准上有效消除重影，并取得更优的结构连贯性与时间平滑性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 显式解耦几何对齐与生成去噪的"先传输后去噪"范式，可在免调优条件下显著提升图像变形的结构一致性与过渡平滑度。

Abstract: Image morphing aims to produce a smooth and semantically consistent transition between two input images. Existing diffusion-based morphing methods either require expensive per-pair optimization or rely on implicit spatial alignment, which easily fails under large layout discrepancies. To address these limitations, we propose AlignMorph, a novel tuning-free diffusion framework guided by the principle of transport-then-denoise. We explicitly decouple geometric alignment from generative denoising to avoid structural entanglement. Our framework consists of two core components. (1) Global Semantic Transport, which achieves diffusion-compatible semantic alignment via entropic optimal transport and reliability-aware latent warping; and (2) Coordinate-Aligned Generation, which uses a symmetric bi-phase attention handoff to maintain consistent spatial coordinates throughout denoising. Without any tuning, AlignMorph effectively eliminates ghosting and achieves superior structural coherence and temporal smoothness on morphing benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/51xOne/Alignmorph.

</details>


### [164] [LiAuto-MindViT: A Hybrid Vision Backbone with Adaptive Bidirectional Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24337)
*Lifu Mu,Shuai Chen,Wen Zheng,Haoyi Sun,Xueyang Fu,Pengfei Yu,Ning Mao,Tao Wei,Zhou Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiAuto-MindViT混合视觉骨干，融合CNN、Mamba与Transformer，通过自适应双向Mamba和重参数化ConvSE实现高效推理与多任务SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba模型在长序列建模上潜力强，但直接用于视觉存在困难：视觉理解需要局部邻域相关性和多方向空间上下文。现有单向SSM存在方向偏置，且多路径路由开销大。

Method: 核心是自适应双向Mamba（ABM），通过带可学习alpha混合的双向选择性扫描，消除单向SSM的方向偏置，实现内容自适应的方向融合，且无需穷举多路径路由。另提出面向部署的重参数化ConvSE（RepConvSE）模块，利用结构重参数化降低延迟与内存访问开销。整体为CNN、Mamba、Transformer协同的混合骨干。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测和语义分割任务上取得SOTA性能，并通过重参数化实现高效推理。

Conclusion: LiAuto-MindViT证明了CNN、Mamba与Transformer混合设计在视觉任务中的有效性，兼顾性能与推理效率，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: While Mamba-based models have shown strong potential for long sequence modeling, adapting them to vision is challenging due to the requirement of local neighborhood correlations and multi-directional spatial contexts for visual understanding. In this paper, we present LiAuto-MindViT, a novel hybrid vision backbone that synergizes the strengths of CNNs, Mamba, and Transformers. The core of our design is the Adaptive Bidirectional Mamba (ABM), which eliminates the directional bias of unidirectional SSMs through bidirectional selective scanning with learnable alpha blending, enabling content-adaptive directional fusion without the overhead of exhaustive multi-path routing. To further accelerate inference, we propose a deployment-friendly Reparameterized ConvSE (RepConvSE) module that leverages structural reparameterization to reduce latency and memory access overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LiAuto-MindViT achieves state-of-the-art performance on image classification, object detection, and semantic segmentation while enabling efficient inference through reparameterization.

</details>


### [165] [Dissecting Agentic Forensics: The Role of Triage, Prompting, and Evidence Arbitration in Open-World Fake Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24359)
*Xianlong Li,Pietro Bongini,Niccoló Pancino,Marco Blanchini,Benedetta Tondi,Mauro Barni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究免训练的智能体式图像取证框架，发现推理能力（而非检测能力）才是开放世界取证的核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像取证正演变为开放世界问题：篡改类型涵盖全合成图像到局部编辑、拼接与替换，而多数取证检测器仍只针对单一篡改类型。智能体式AI被视为有前景的方案，理论上可评估单个检测器可靠性、识别超范围证据、仲裁冲突报告，但目前尚不清楚哪些组件真正驱动性能，以及其收益能否在分布偏移下保持。

Method: 作者研究一个免训练的智能体框架，围绕专家检测器、逐检测器分诊和冲突感知的证据仲裁构建。采用六种配置与三种多模态大语言模型主干，在分布内与分布外数据上剖析分诊、提示与推理质量的作用。

Result: 朴素检测器融合在真实图像上存在严重的假阳性率。分诊与提示能通过过滤不可靠证据、暴露检测器局限而稳定提升性能。但主导因素是推理本身：更强的评判模型显著优于较弱者，在分布偏移下尤为明显。最引人注目的是，各配置下篡改召回率几乎饱和。

Conclusion: 开放世界图像取证的主要挑战并非检测篡改，而是校准对专用取证工具的信任并仲裁相互冲突的证据。

Abstract: Image forensics is increasingly an open-world problem: manipulations range from fully synthetic images to localized edits, splicing and swapping, while most forensic detectors remain specialized to a single manipulation family. Agentic AI has recently emerged as a promising solution. In principle, such systems can assess the reliability of individual detectors, identify out-of-scope evidence, and arbitrate conflicting reports. However, it remains unclear which components actually drive performance and whether their benefits persist under distribution shift. To answer these questions, we study a training-free agentic framework built around specialist detectors, per-detector triage, and conflict-aware evidence arbitration. Using six configurations and three multimodal large language model backbones, we dissect the role of triage, prompting, and reasoning quality on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data. Our results show that naive detector fusion suffers from severe false-positive rates on authentic images. Triage and prompting consistently improve performance by filtering unreliable evidence and exposing detector limitations. However, the dominant factor is represented by reasoning itself: A stronger judge substantially outperforms a weaker one, particularly under distribution shift. Most notably, manipulation recall is nearly saturated across all configurations, indicating that the main challenge of open-world image forensics is not detecting manipulations, but calibrating trust in specialized forensic tools and arbitrating conflicting evidence.

</details>


### [166] [TReViS: Temporal Repetition Structure Aware Video Synthesis for Self-supervised Repetitive Action Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24367)
*Fanqi Yu,Shengming Ma,Stefano Fiorini,Vito Paolo Pastore,Xuan Qi,Vittorio Murino,Cigdem Beyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TReViS自监督视频合成框架，利用时序自相似矩阵推断重复结构并合成带伪标签的训练视频，使重复动作计数无需任何标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全监督重复动作计数（RAC）性能虽好，但依赖密集时序标注，标注成本高且难以规模化；因此需要一种无需重复标签的训练方案。

Method: TReViS通过时序自相似矩阵（Temporal Self-Similarity Matrix）估计无标签视频底层的时序重复结构，推断其周期统计信息，并据此合成既保留真实重复模式、又引入可控时序变化的新训练序列；合成视频配以伪标签，用于从头训练现有RAC架构。

Result: 在多个数据集和骨干网络上，TReViS持续优于先前的自监督方法，并与若干监督基线性能相当，同时保持完全无标签。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结构感知的视频合成对无标签重复动作计数有效，为摆脱密集标注提供了可行路径。代码已开源。

Abstract: Fully supervised repetitive action counting (RAC) has achieved strong performance, but requires dense temporal annotations that are costly and difficult to scale. We propose TReViS, a self-supervised video synthesis framework that enables training RAC models without any repetition labels. TReViS estimates the underlying temporal repetition structure of an unlabeled video via a Temporal Self-Similarity Matrix, infers its cycle statistics, and synthesizes new training sequences that preserve realistic repetition patterns while introducing controlled temporal variability. These synthesized videos are paired with pseudo-labels and used to train existing RAC architectures from scratch. Across multiple datasets and backbones, TReViS consistently outperforms prior self-supervised methods and achieves performance competitive with several supervised baselines, while remaining fully label-free, demonstrating the effectiveness of structure-aware video synthesis for label-free RAC. The source code is available at https://github.com/yfqi/TReViS.

</details>


### [167] [Topographic Training Concentrates Causal Circuits Without Improving Neuron Monosemanticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24379)
*Gautam Ranka,Shubham Santosh Pandere,Aiden Dsouza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过在训练时引入空间局部性损失（TopoLoss）作为轻量先验，可提升ViT的可解释性，但增益体现在电路层面而非单个神经元层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer的机制可解释性旨在将模型计算分解为人类可读单元，但神经元表征纠缠多概念，特征叠加是核心障碍。现有缓解方法（稀疏自编码器、字典学习）多为事后处理，不改变底层网络。作者希望探究训练时的空间局部性损失能否作为轻量先验，提升标准可解释性工具的效果。

Method: 在ImageNet-100上训练ViT，设置多个TopoLoss权重α；用激活补丁（activation patching）测量地形簇的因果充分性，并用稀疏自编码器拟合同一残差流以分析特征几何。

Result: α=1.0时，地形簇的因果充分性比同规模随机单元集合高2.79倍，且随α单调增强；SAE的L0稀疏度下降11%，死特征比例上升19倍，但标准神经元级单义性评分不变。

Conclusion: 地形压力作用于电路层面，将因果质量集中于空间局部结构，却未解缠单个神经元。这一分离表明当前神经元级单义性指标对某些真实可解释性增益不敏感，廉价架构先验可作为事后工具的训练时补充。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of vision transformers seeks to decompose model computation into human-readable units, but learned representations entangle many concepts in each neuron. Feature superposition is widely treated as the central obstacle to this decomposition, yet most mitigations (sparse autoencoders, dictionary learning) are post-hoc and leave the underlying network unchanged. We ask whether a spatial-locality training loss (TopoLoss) can act as a lightweight, training-time prior that improves interpretability of standard mech-interp tools. Training ViT on ImageNet-100 across multiple TopoLoss weights $α$, we measure causal sufficiency of topographic clusters via activation patching and feature geometry via sparse autoencoders fit to the same residual stream. At $α=1.0$, topographic clusters are 2.79$\times$ more causally sufficient than random unit sets of the same size, with the effect increasing monotonically in $α$. SAE L0 sparsity decreases by 11% and dead-feature fraction rises 19-fold, yet standard neuron-level monosemanticity scores are unchanged, indicating that topographic pressure acts at circuit level, concentrating causal mass into spatially local structures without disentangling individual neurons. This dissociation suggests current neuron-level monosemanticity metrics are insensitive to a class of real interpretability gains, and positions cheap architectural priors as a viable training-time complement to post-hoc tooling.

</details>


### [168] [A Lightweight Convolutional Neural Network for Real-Time Recognition of Hand-Drawn Geometric Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24384)
*Shahir Abdullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一个桌面应用，使用约9.8万参数的小型CNN识别手绘的圆形、正方形、矩形和三角形，在自建2000张28x28图像数据集上达到96.01%验证准确率，并公开代码、模型与数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手绘几何形状识别是草图识别的基础子问题，在教育、人机交互和图表数字化中有应用价值；同时需要一个轻量、可复现且带有交互界面的识别系统来验证小规模数据下的可行性。

Method: 独立采集并公开2000张28x28像素的标注形状图像；构建紧凑CNN：三个卷积块（16/32/64滤波器）配合最大池化、模型内数据增强（随机水平翻转、旋转、缩放）、dropout正则化的128单元全连接层和4类线性输出层，共97,956个可训练参数；使用Adam优化器，直接在logits上计算稀疏分类交叉熵损失；按80/20划分训练与验证集；采用Tkinter图形界面支持鼠标绘图并即时输出类别与置信度。

Result: 在80/20训练-验证划分下，模型训练准确率为94.80%，验证准确率为96.01%，验证损失为0.1437。

Conclusion: 该系统能够以小型CNN实现较高的手绘几何形状识别准确率，并提供即时交互预测；论文将其置于草图与形状识别文献中，对比了相关手绘形状分类研究的准确率，同时讨论了小型、单一贡献者数据集的局限性，并公开全部源代码、训练模型和分类数据集以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Recognizing hand-drawn geometric shapes is a foundational sub-problem of sketch recognition, with applications in education, human-computer interaction, and diagram digitization. This paper presents the design, implementation, and evaluation of a desktop application that recognizes four basic hand-drawn geometric shapes, circle, square, rectangle, and triangle using a compact Convolutional Neural Network (CNN). A dataset of 2,000 labeled 28x28-pixel shape images was collected independently and released publicly. The classifier consists of three convolutional blocks (16, 32, and 64 filters) with max-pooling, an in-model data-augmentation stage (random horizontal flip, rotation, and zoom), a dropout-regularized dense layer of 128 units, and a 4-way linear output layer, totaling 97{,}956 trainable parameters. The network is trained with the Adam optimizer on a sparse categorical cross-entropy objective computed directly on logits. On an 80/20 train-validation split, the model achieves 94.80% training accuracy and 96.01% validation accuracy with a validation loss of 0.1437. A Tkinter-based graphical interface allows a user to draw a shape with the mouse and receive an immediate class prediction with a confidence score. We situate this system within the broader sketch and shape-recognition literature, compare its accuracy against related hand-drawn shape classification studies, and discuss the limitations inherent to a small, single-contributor dataset. The complete source code, trained model, and per-class datasets are released publicly to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [169] [Can Spiking Neural Networks play pinball? A neuromorphic motion detector for target tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24403)
*Mazdak Fatahi,Šárka Pryjmaková,Pierre Boulet,Giulia D'Angelo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个全脉冲、实时的感知-行动闭环弹球系统，用动态视觉传感器与SpiNNaker上的脉冲时间差编码器联合估计小球的位置、速度与方向，命中率达56.1%，反应时21.7 ms，功耗约148 μW，并使用少于25k神经元，展示了高效 neuromorphic 实时感知-行动的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物视觉系统能以稀疏、异步脉冲信号实现连续低延迟运动感知，在严格能量约束下完成实时追踪。事件相机仿照哺乳动物视网膜，仅捕捉局部亮度变化，为脉冲神经网络提供天然计算基础。弹球任务需要精确运动估计与快速反应，是受控但动态的测试平台，用以验证事件驱动、脉冲式计算在闭环感知-行动中的实时性与能效优势。

Method: 构建全脉冲、实时闭环弹球感知-行动流水线：动态视觉传感器观测快速移动的小球；在SpiNNaker神经形态平台上，使用脉冲时间差编码器网络联合估计小球位置、速度与方向。针对实时运行，系统在感受野大小、累积窗口与角度调谐宽度上进行表征分析。设计两套逐渐增加物理真实性的挡板机制，并在闭环中与人类玩家基准对比。进一步通过调节单一可解释策略参数来复现不同人类打法。最后使用物理演示器，追踪真实小球并闭环驱动真实挡板。

Result: 系统命中率达56.1%，约为人类平均水平的两倍；反应时间21.7 ms，其中网络延迟5 ms；功耗估计约148 μW；使用少于25k神经元。在更真实的挡板动力学下，仅调节一个可解释策略参数即可复现从谨慎到激进的全谱人类打法，且无需改变感知流水线。物理演示器验证了该原理可超越仿真运行。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一个全脉冲、免学习、紧凑且高能效的实时神经形态感知-行动闭环系统，在速度与能效方面处于事件驱动闭环演示的领先水平，并证明其可从仿真迁移到真实物理平台。

Abstract: Biological visual systems achieve continuous, low-latency motion perception by processing sparse, asynchronous spiking signals, enabling real-time tracking under strict energy constraints. Event-based cameras, inspired by the mammalian retina, replicate this efficiency by capturing only local brightness changes as asynchronous events, offering a natural substrate for spiking neural networks (SNNs) to parallelise computation and adapt to fast-changing scenes. Pinball provides a controlled yet dynamic testbed, requiring precise motion estimation and fast reaction to a small, rapidly moving target.
  This work presents a fully spiking, real-time perception-to-action pipeline for closed-loop pinball gameplay. A dynamic vision sensor observes a small, fast-moving ball, and a network of spiking Time-Difference Encoders on the SpiNNaker neuromorphic platform jointly estimates its position, speed, and direction. The system is characterised across receptive field size, accumulation window, and angular tuning width for real-time operation, and benchmarked in closed loop against human players across two flipper regimes of increasing physical realism. It achieves a hit rate of 56.1%, nearly double the human average, reacting within 21.7 ms (5 ms network latency) and consuming an estimated 148 μW using fewer than 25k neurons, among the fastest and most energy-efficient event-based closed-loop demonstrators benchmarked. Under more realistic flipper dynamics, tuning a single interpretable policy parameter reproduces the full spectrum of human play styles, from cautious to aggressive, with no change to the perception pipeline. A physical demonstrator, tracking a real ball and actuating real flippers in closed loop, confirms the principle operates beyond simulation. Its fully spiking, learning-free design offers a compact, energy-efficient example of real-time neuromorphic perception-to-action.

</details>


### [170] [DeCo: Efficient Decouple-to-Couple Learning for Multi-Task Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24409)
*Xiaoqiang Lu,Licheng Jiao,Long Sun,Yuting Yang,Xu Liu,Lingling Li,Wenping Ma,Fang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeCo框架，通过任务特定表示解耦与互补先验耦合两阶段范式，解决多任务视觉定位中的任务冲突问题，在多个自然与遥感基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务视觉定位需同时理解语言语义并完成精确定位与分割，但现有方法多以共享表示实现任务协作，忽略了定位与分割任务在特征兴趣上的内在冲突，导致表示干扰。

Method: 提出Decouple-to-Couple（DeCo）两阶段框架：1）任务感知语义解耦（TSD），在显著词级引导下将共享视觉线索路由为独立特征，缓解定位与分割的表示干扰；2）混合先验耦合（HPC），利用分割的密集监督提供定位先验，融合句子级语义先验与掩码空间先验。基于冻结的多模态编码器，仅需轻量可训练参数。

Result: 在RefCOCO/+/G-Ref、ReferIt、Flickr、DIOR-RSVG、SARVG1.0、RRSIS-D、RIS-LAD、RefDIOR等基准上，DeCo在自然与遥感两个场景均取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: DeCo通过解耦-耦合的两阶段设计有效缓解任务冲突，并借助掩码先验增强定位，在参数高效与强泛化性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Multi-task visual grounding requires models to jointly understand linguistic semantics and perform accurate visual localization and segmentation. Despite the success of multimodal large language models, effectively adapting them to multiple grounding objectives remains challenging. Existing methods commonly enforce task cooperation through shared representations, while overlooking the intrinsic conflict between task-oriented feature interests. In this paper, we introduce $\textbf{DeCo}$, an efficient $\textbf{De}$couple-to-$\textbf{Co}$uple learning framework that resolves this dilemma through a two-stage paradigm: task-specific representation decoupling followed by complementary prior coupling. Specifically, we first propose Task-aware Semantic Decoupling (TSD) to route shared visual cues into individual features under salient word-level guidance, alleviating representation interference between localization and segmentation. Furthermore, we observe that segmentation naturally provides informative localization priors due to dense supervision. Based on this insight, we introduce Hybrid Prior Coupling (HPC), which integrates sentence-level semantic prior with mask-derived spatial prior for enhanced grounding. Built upon a frozen multimodal encoder, DeCo requires lightweight trainable parameters while achieving strong generalization across multiple grounding objectives. Extensive experiments on RefCOCO/+, G-Ref, ReferIt, Flickr, DIOR-RSVG, SARVG1.0, RRSIS-D, RIS-LAD, and RefDIOR demonstrate that DeCo achieves state-of-the-art performance on both natural and remote sensing benchmarks. The code and models are available at https://github.com/xiaoqiang-lu/DeCo.

</details>


### [171] [Estimating Accurate Hand Pose in Camera Space with Vision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24424)
*Kaiwen Ren,Yiran Jiang,Yongjing Ye,Shihong Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CS-ViT框架，通过变换同构监督与透视信息嵌入解决单目全局手部姿态估计中的深度模糊与耦合问题，在HO3D上CS-MJE指标最高超越SOTA 37.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目RGB全局手部姿态估计需同时预测手腕在相机坐标系中的位置，面临两大挑战：(1)单目深度模糊；(2)透视投影中手部局部姿态与手腕全局位置的耦合效应——投影由局部姿态、手腕位置与相机内参共同决定。现有方法难以有效解耦并提取手部深度信息。

Method: 在主流编码器-解码器架构中集成两项创新：(1)Transformation-Isomorphism Supervision，用于手部深度信息提取；(2)Perspective Information Embedding，用于解耦局部姿态与手腕位置的耦合效应。此外提出帧率感知的多数据集训练策略，用于序列姿态精化。

Result: 在HO3D数据集上，完整集成方法在CS-MJE指标上相较SOTA最高提升37.1%。

Conclusion: 变换同构监督与透视信息嵌入能有效应对深度模糊与投影耦合问题，配合帧率感知多数据集训练策略，可显著提升单目全局手部姿态估计精度。项目页面已公开。

Abstract: Monocular RGB-based hand pose estimation has emerged as a critical research frontier in computer vision. The local hand pose estimation methods predict hand poses relative to the wrist, while global hand pose estimation also requires estimating the wrist's position in the camera coordinate system. However, this camera-space estimation confronts two fundamental challenges: (1) depth ambiguity in monocular settings, and (2) the coupling effect of hand local poses and global wrist positions in the perspective projections. In particular, this coupling reflects that the projections are jointly determined by local hand poses, wrist positions, and camera intrinsics. To overcome these challenges, our framework proposes two key innovations: Transformation-Isomorphism Supervision for hand-depth information extraction and Perspective Information Embedding for resolving above coupling effect of local pose and wrist position, both integrated within the mainstream encoder-decoder architecture. Besides, we propose a novel framerate-aware multi-dataset training strategy for sequential pose refinement. Our fully integrated approach achieves at most 37.1\% superiority in CS-MJE over SOTA on HO3D. Project page: https://github.com/Mine268/CS-ViT.

</details>


### [172] [Do LiDAR Language Models Really Understand Spatio-temporal Relationships?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24452)
*Runyi Yang,Murat Akkoyun,Di Wen,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Junwei Zheng,Xiaoye Wang,Kailun Yang,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LiDAR-Hallu 基准与诊断协议，揭示 4D LiDAR 语言模型在时空推理上被聚合准确率掩盖的严重失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D LiDAR 语言模型评测发现，一直选同一选项几乎能达到两个 B4DL 衍生配置的准确率，说明传统多项选择聚合指标无法真实反映模型是否理解物体及其演变的空间关系。

Method: 构建 LiDAR-Hallu：基于 150 个 nuScenes 场景的 10,000 道题，覆盖物体存在性、自车相对位置、距离排序、相对运动、时间定位，并给出明确的对象选择、时间比较与参考答案判定规则；诊断协议包含固定答案与候选内容控制、提示相同但参考答案相反的跨场景配对、以及关系特定召回分析。

Result: 分析 100,000 条回复：候选时长本身即可在未观察 LiDAR 时预测时间答案；配对问题上模型对参考相反的场景常给相同答案；两种配置在所有测试条件下均未命中任何正向横向运动样本；时间打乱对比解码净收益甚微，修复被新增错误抵消且主要失败依旧存在。

Conclusion: 时空推理评测须考察模型能否区分所查询的物理关系，而非仅依赖单答案准确率。代码、检查点与数据已开源。

Abstract: Recent 4D LiDAR language models aim to reason about objects and their evolving spatial relationships. Yet, in our evaluation, always selecting the same option nearly matches the multiple-choice accuracy of two B4DL-derived configurations. We introduce LiDAR-Hallu, a geometry-referenced benchmark and diagnostic protocol with 10,000 questions across 150 nuScenes scenes. It covers object existence, ego-relative position, distance ordering, relative motion, and temporal localization, with explicit rules for selecting objects, comparing times, and determining reference answers. Our protocol combines fixed-answer and candidate-content controls, cross-scene pairs with identical prompts but opposite reference answers, and relation-specific recall. Analysis of 100,000 recorded responses reveals failures hidden by aggregate accuracy. Candidate duration alone makes temporal answers predictable without observing LiDAR. On paired questions, the models frequently give the same answer to scenes requiring opposite answers. Relation-specific analysis further shows that both configurations miss every positive lateral-motion case across all tested conditions. Temporal-shuffle contrastive decoding provides little net improvement, as repairs are largely offset by new errors and the main failures persist. These results show that evaluating spatio-temporal reasoning requires testing whether models distinguish the queried physical relationships, rather than relying on individual-answer accuracy alone. The source code, checkpoints, and data are released at https://github.com/Awesome4D/4DMLLM_Hallucination_Bench.

</details>


### [173] [MECAIL: Communication-Aware Incremental Learning for Object Detection with 14.6 KB Spatiotemporal Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24455)
*Matthias Neuwirth-Trapp,Maarten Bieshaar,Danda Paudel,Konrad Schindler,Luc Van Gool,Christos Sakaridis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MECAIL方法，用通信感知的专家混合增量学习，在每模块14.6KB严格限制下实现边缘设备适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能交通系统的增量学习需求受限于边缘设备算力不足，需从服务器下发更新。但传输受TCP/UDP/BTP及V2X/Wi-Fi/2G-5G带宽约束，需要极小的模块尺寸来保证可靠传输并实现细粒度时空场景覆盖。

Method: 将每个新领域或环境交由一个小型专家网络，通过专家混合框架适配固定基础模型；设定每模块14.6KB上限以适配首个TCP窗口并减少UDP/BTP分片。

Result: 在D-RICO和ODinW-13数据集上验证，性能基本匹配参数密集型方法，同时支持高带宽效率的大规模实际部署。

Conclusion: MECAIL满足严格通信约束，可为高度特定、聚焦且临时的场景提供专家全面覆盖，推动边缘增量学习的实用化部署。

Abstract: Intelligent transportation systems require Incremental Learning (IL) to continually improve their overall performance in dynamic environments. However, most edge devices lack the computational resources to support on-device IL, requiring updates to be transmitted from centralized servers. We propose using this setup to obtain dense, specialized module coverage that adapts a fixed base model to specific spatiotemporal contexts, such as parking lots, gas stations, ferries, or construction sites. However, in order to reliably transmit these modules to the edge device, using TCP, UDP, and BTP over V2X, Wi-Fi, and 2G-5G hardware, we establish a strict limit of 14.6 KB per module to fit within the first TCP window and to minimize UDP/BTP fragmentation. We further introduce Mixture-of-Experts for Communication-Aware Incremental Learning (MECAIL), the first method that meets this strict requirement, in which each new domain or environment is served by a small expert network that adapts the base model. We validate MECAIL on D-RICO and ODinW-13, where it largely matches the performance of parameter-heavy approaches while enabling practical, bandwidth-efficient large-scale deployment. This allows comprehensive coverage by experts for highly specific, focused, and temporary situations.

</details>


### [174] [MIGA:Shared-Geometry Gaussian Representation with Implicit Amplitude Modeling for Accelerated 3D Multi-Echo MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24468)
*Jingran Xu,Yuanyuan Liu,Yanjie Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MIGA，一种针对欠采样三维多回波 MRI 的扫描特异性重建框架，结合共享各向异性高斯几何、坐标条件多输出幅值网络和显式回波相位变量，无需全采样训练数据即可实现高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维多回波 MRI 虽提供丰富解剖与定量信息，但重复体编码导致采集时间过长，需要 k 空间欠采样；而重建欠采样多回波数据必须同时利用共享解剖结构并保留回波相关信号变化，且全容积建模带来巨大的计算与内存开销。因此需要一种兼顾共享与回波差异、计算高效的重建方法。

Method: MIGA 由三部分组成：共享各向异性高斯几何、坐标条件多输出幅值网络、显式回波相位变量。高斯几何为各回波提供共同空间支撑，隐式网络建模空间结构化幅值变化，相位变量保留回波特异的复信号信息。所有组件仅利用已采集的多线圈 k 空间联合优化，无需全采样训练数据。

Result: 实验表明，MIGA 在多种成像任务和加速倍数下均持续优于对比方法，且欠采样越强提升越大；在评估的全容积多回波方法中也取得了较好的质量—成本平衡。

Conclusion: 结果支持将共享高斯几何与隐式回波相关幅值建模相结合，用于加速三维多回波 MRI 重建的有效性。
