TL;DR: 本文对多语言语音识别中的联邦学习进行了全面基准测试，比较了多种Speech-LLM架构、FedAvg与FedProx，并发现分组件调优学习率及全三组件适配能取得最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐私敏感的分布式环境中部署多语言Speech-LLM需要联邦学习支持，但目前缺乏对相关架构和算法在联邦场景下的系统性评估与设计指导。

Method: 基于Multilingual LibriSpeech数据集，评测四种Speech-LLM架构，比较FedAvg与FedProx在冻结/非冻结编码器配置下的表现，并分别对语音编码器、连接器和解码器的学习率进行独立调优，采用LoRA（编码器与解码器）加连接器全训练的三组件适配方案。

Result: 独立调优各部分学习率可得到最低错误率；全三组件适配取得最佳联邦学习结果；FedProx的效果依赖架构，在多语言预训练架构（如EuroLLM对比TinyLlama，固定编码器时）优势明显。

Conclusion: LLM骨干容量在调节对异构数据分布的鲁棒性中起关键作用，研究结果为在隐私敏感的分布式环境中部署多语言Speech-LLM提供了具体的设计指导。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive benchmark of Federated Learning (FL) for multilingual Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR), evaluating four Speech-LLM architectures on the Multilingual LibriSpeech dataset. We compare FedAvg and FedProx across frozen and unfrozen encoder configurations, demonstrating that optimized learning rates are critical for performance. Specifically, independently tuning the learning rates for the speech encoder, connector, and decoder yields the lowest error rates, with full three-component adaptation (LoRA for encoder and decoder, full training for the connector) producing the best FL results. We observe that FedProx efficacy is architecture-dependent, providing notable advantages in multilingual pre-trained architectures (e.g., EuroLLM over TinyLlama when keeping the encoder fixed); this indicates that LLM backbone capacity plays a key role in mediating resilience to heterogeneous data distributions. These findings offer concrete design guidance for deploying multilingual Speech-LLMs in privacy-sensitive, distributed environments.

</details>


### [349] [From UNDRR Reports to Event Records: Schema-Constrained LLM Extraction of Georeferenced Disasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23853)
*Camilla Andreozzi,Phuong-Anh Nguyen-Le,Zhijing Jin,Revati Mani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个LLM流水线，从减灾档案的散文文本中生成符合受控灾害词表与固定模式的地理参照事件记录，并保留证据以供审核。应用于PreventionWeb的1万份文档，产出3572条记录，覆盖24种灾害类型，81%的地点提及解析到OpenStreetMap几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EM-DAT等灾害数据库无法直接摄取减灾档案中以散文形式描述的危险事件，导致大量档案信息难以结构化入库。

Method: 采用LLM流水线，基于受控灾害词表和固定模式生成候选地理参照事件记录并保留证据；在PreventionWeb的10000份文档上应用，并以分层217份文档参考集中171个人工正样本窗口进行评测，与spaCy-gazetteer基线对比，同时比较十种LLM。

Result: 共从1913份文档产出3572条记录，覆盖24种灾害类型，81%地点提及成功解析；GPT-5在171个正样本窗口上取得86.0%汇总属性F1，基线为44.2%；GPT-5.4在十种LLM中排名最高（86.6% F1）；逐字证据出现率为GPT-5的72.0%与GPT-5.4的47.2%。

Conclusion: 该流水线可从减灾档案中高效抽取地理参照灾害事件记录并保持可追溯证据，同时报告了生产失败模式及自动化标签与位置规则合规检查；提示、模式与输出将公开，以便适配各国报告档案。

Abstract: Disaster-risk-reduction archives describe hazard events in prose that databases such as EM-DAT (Delforge et al., 2025) cannot ingest directly. We present an LLM pipeline that generates candidate georeferenced event records using a controlled hazard vocabulary and fixed schema, retaining evidence for review. Applied to 10,000 documents from PreventionWeb, the knowledge hub managed by UNDRR, it produced 3,572 records from 1,913 documents across 24 hazard types and resolved 81% of location mentions to OpenStreetMap geometries. On 171 human-positive document windows from a stratified 217-document reference set, GPT-5 achieved 86.0% pooled attribute $F_1$, versus 44.2% for the spaCy-gazetteer baseline. Evaluation pools hazard families, location strings, and event years within documents, without assessing their assignment to individual events. GPT-5.4 ranked highest among ten LLMs (86.6% $F_1$). Verbatim evidence occurrence was 72.0% for GPT-5 and 47.2% for GPT-5.4, measuring textual traceability without establishing attribute support. We report production failure modes and automated label and location-rule compliance checks. Prompts, schema, and outputs will be released for adaptation to national reporting archives.

</details>


### [350] [Q-TIE: A Lightweight and Generalizable Re-ranking Framework for Temporal Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23880)
*Soyeon Kim,Hyunjin Kim,JinYeong Bak,Steven Euijong Whang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-TIE，一种基于可学习时间意图抽取（TIE）的重排序框架，将查询的时间约束映射为统一区间表示，从而兼顾时间检索器与时间重排序器的优势，在多种时间查询类型上优于现有TIR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着RAG的兴起，时间信息检索（TIR）日益重要，因为时间不匹配的证据会严重误导结果。现有两类TIR范式各有短板：时间检索器虽能通过学习表示灵活理解查询，但往往无法显式处理时间约束；时间重排序器虽能更显式地施加时间约束，却常依赖预定义的重排序规则，缺乏泛化性。

Method: 提出Q-TIE重排序框架，其核心是可学习的时间意图抽取（TIE）模型。TIE将每个查询的时间约束映射为统一的区间表示⟨t_start, t_end⟩，通过基于模型的学习摆脱对预定义规则的依赖，同时将时间约束作为独立信号显式建模。

Result: 实验表明，Q-TIE持续优于现有TIR方法，在各类时间查询类型上具有更强的泛化能力，并为时间感知的RAG流程提供了一种轻量且有效的附加组件。

Conclusion: Q-TIE通过将时间约束显式建模为统一区间表示并结合可学习方式，兼顾了时间检索器的灵活性与时间重排序器的约束显式性，实现了两类范式通常相互权衡的目标，为鲁棒TIR提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Temporal Information Retrieval (TIR) has been increasingly critical given the rise of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). Since temporally mismatched evidence can be highly misleading, TIR aims to retrieve documents that are both semantically and temporally relevant to a query. Two TIR paradigms have emerged - temporal retrievers and temporal re-rankers - differing in how temporal relevance is modeled. While these paradigms provide complementary strengths, our analysis reveals that each alone falls short of robust TIR: temporal retrievers provide flexible query understanding via learned representations, but often fail to explicitly account for temporal constraints; temporal re-rankers can enforce such constraints more explicitly, but often rely on predefined re-ranking rules. To address this, we propose Q-TIE, a re-ranking framework based on learned Temporal Intent Extraction (TIE). By introducing a TIE model that maps each query's temporal constraint into a unified interval representation (i.e., $\langle t_{start}, t_{end} \rangle$), Q-TIE generalizes beyond predefined rules via model-based learning while explicitly modeling temporal constraints as a separate signal - jointly achieving what each paradigm typically trades off. Experiments demonstrate that Q-TIE consistently outperforms existing TIR methods with stronger generalizability across temporal query types, and provides a lightweight yet effective add-on for temporally-aware RAG pipelines. Code: https://github.com/ssoy0701/Q-TIE.

</details>


### [351] [this-that-model-1.0: A typed decision model that decides in 30 ms, for a millionth of a cent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23886)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: this-that-model-1.0 是一个 2B 参数的"类型化决策模型"，直接从指定位置的隐藏状态读取答案并限制在调用方声明的选项集合内，不生成任何文本 token，因此在单块消费级/笔记本 GPU 上以约 30.9 ms 完成决策、约 32 次/秒的吞吐，且状态不离开本机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件越来越多地把分支决策（工单进入哪个队列、命令是否可安全执行、索赔能否无人干预通过）委托给模型。程序需要的不是散文式输出，而是 n 个已声明选项之一和一个可阈值化的数值。当前做法要往返调用前沿模型 API：数百毫秒延迟、按 token 计费、还需额外解析器，而问题本身往往只是三个子句的合取，成本与收益严重不匹配。

Method: 提出 2B 参数的类型化决策模型：答案直接从指定位置的隐状态读出，并被限制在调用方声明的选项集合内；不生成任何文本，因此不存在格式错误；同一请求中的所有问题在同一次前向传播中一起作答。模型已开源（https://huggingface.co/flock-io/this-that-model-1.0）。

Result: 单块笔记本 GPU 上决策耗时 30.9 ms、生成 0 个输出 token，而前沿 API 调用需 8758 ms；托管系统回答此类问题良好时每题需先生成 21–212 个 token 并逐一计费。单块消费级 GPU 上吞吐 32 次决策/秒，状态不出本机。在第三方记录的 68 道决策题队列上（沿用其输入与措辞），得分为 0.941、Brier 分数 0.042，对比托管服务 Jev 的 0.765 与 0.133。内部 42 个任务族套件跑一遍需 32 秒、电费 0.000217 美元；测得最准确的托管模型需 155.2 分钟与 10.636 美元。局限方面：多步算术（单次前向传播无法携带中间结果）得分 0.560，对方为 0.98–1.00；一轮针对性二次训练只提升了它专为设计的 5 个任务族，对其余 13 个任务族零迁移。

Conclusion: 在"少量声明选项 + 可阈值数值"的类型化决策场景中，把答案读作隐状态而非生成文本，可在准确率上不逊甚至优于部分托管服务，同时把延迟、成本与 token 开销降低数个数量级，并保持本地部署与隐私。其能力边界明确：需要多步中间推理的任务族不适合单次前向传播；针对性微调收益高度局限于目标任务族，难以跨族迁移。

Abstract: Software delegates more of its branches to models every year: which queue a ticket enters, whether a command is safe to run, whether a claim clears without a person. What the program needs back is not prose. It is one of n declared options and a number it can threshold. Today that costs a round trip to a frontier model -- hundreds of milliseconds, a per-token bill, and a parser -- for a question that is usually a conjunction of three clauses. this-that-model-1.0 is a 2B-parameter typed decision model. Its answer is read directly from the hidden state at a designated position and restricted to the option set the caller declared, so no text is generated, nothing can be malformed, and every question in a request is answered in the same forward pass. It decides in 30.9 ms on one laptop GPU and generates zero output tokens doing it, where a frontier API call costs 8758 ms and the hosted systems that answer these questions well spend between 21 and 212 generated tokens per question thinking first, billed for every one. It sustains 32 decisions per second on one consumer GPU and never lets the state leave the machine. On a third party's recorded cohort of 68 decision questions, on their inputs and their wording, it scores 0.941 with a Brier score of 0.042, against 0.765 and 0.133 for the hosted service Jev on the same items. One pass of our 42-family internal suite takes 32 seconds and 0.000217 USD of electricity; the most accurate hosted model we measured needs 155.2 minutes and 10.636 USD. We also report where it loses. On multi-step arithmetic, which a single forward pass cannot carry intermediate results through, it scores 0.560 against their 0.98 to 1.00, and a targeted second training round improved the five task families it was written for and transferred to none of the other 13. The model is open-sourced in https://huggingface.co/flock-io/this-that-model-1.0

</details>


### [352] [Time-Incremental Continued Pretraining of LLMs: Knowledge Updates Without Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23916)
*Fırat Öncel,Salman Hussain Ali,Mirco Ravanelli,Cem Subakan,Çağatay Yıldız*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究真实场景下的"时间增量式持续预训练"(CPT)：在严格晚于各模型知识截止时间的 FineWeb-Edu 数据上对六个开源模型（OLMo2、Llama-3.1/3.2、Gemma-3-1B，1B–8B）继续预训练，发现知识确可习得且无灾难性遗忘，通用能力几乎无损，数据质量优于数量，LoRA 在足够秩时可替代全量 CPT，且增益可通过 SFT 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在预训练结束即开始过时，而从零重训成本极高。持续预训练是自然解法，但已有评估多沿用"持续学习"视角、假设数据流不相交，这与网页规模爬取的时间增量更新（相邻快照存在大量 URL 重叠）不符，因此需要在此真实设定下重新审视 CPT 的效果与代价。

Method: 在严格晚于每个模型知识截止时间的 FineWeb-Edu 数据快照上进行继续预训练，覆盖 3 个模型家族、4 个参数规模共 6 个开源模型，并从四个实际问题（知识是否习得、代价多大、配方如何、能否承受部署）组织实验，比较数据规模与质量、学习率、LoRA 秩、以及 SFT/DPO 后续阶段的影响。

Result: (i) 知识可习得但异质，6 个模型中 5 个在截止前事实回忆上也有提升，且无灾难性遗忘，增益与预训练饱和程度（主要由每参数 token 预算驱动）相关；(ii) 十三任务套件宏平均相对基线波动在 0.01 以内，通用能力代价极小；(iii) 数据质量主导数量（精选 6B token 可匹配更广的 40B），知识获取与通用能力的最优学习率相差约一个数量级，足够秩的 LoRA 可媲美全量 CPT；(iv) CPT 增益可通过 SFT 迁移，DPO 效果依模型家族而异。

Conclusion: 在时间增量式 CPT 的真实设定下，结果比以往持续学习文献所暗示的更为乐观：模型能获得新知识、几乎不损失通用能力，并可经下游对齐阶段迁移，为低成本知识更新提供了可行配方。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) drift out of date the moment their pretraining ends, yet retraining from scratch is prohibitively expensive. Continued pretraining (CPT) is the natural remedy, but it is typically evaluated through a continual learning lens that assumes disjoint data streams. This is a poor fit for time-incremental updates on web-scale crawls, where successive snapshots share substantial URL overlap by design. We study time-incremental CPT in this realistic regime: continued pretraining on FineWeb-Edu dumps drawn strictly from after each model's knowledge cutoff, evaluated across six open-weight models spanning three families (OLMo2, Llama-3.1/3.2, Gemma-3-1B) and four parameter scales (1B-3B-7B-8B).
  We organize our findings around four practical questions. (i) Is knowledge acquired? Yes, but heterogeneously, and without catastrophic forgetting: five of six models also improve on pre-cutoff factual recall, and the gains track pretraining saturation (driven primarily by token budget per parameter). (ii) What does it cost? Almost nothing: the macro-average across a thirteen-task suite stays within 0.01 of the base for every model. (iii) What is the recipe? Data quality dominates quantity (a curated 6B-token slice matches a broader 40B one); the optima for knowledge acquisition and general capability are separated by roughly an order of magnitude in learning rate; and LoRA at sufficient rank matches full CPT. (iv) Does it survive deployment? CPT gains transfer through SFT, while DPO's effect is family-dependent. Together, these results paint a more optimistic picture of time-incremental CPT than the prior continual learning literature suggests.

</details>


### [353] [Measuring the Assistant's Harmlessness Preferences on the User Turn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23935)
*Jord Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 后训练并非只给模型加了一层"助手人格"外壳：助手"偏好无害任务"的安全偏好会泛化到用户轮（助手并不发言之处），影响模型对用户发言的预测，且该现象由后训练产生、随规模增长、可被狭窄微调改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 若助手人设只在助手自己的发言轮生效，其偏好应只影响助手说什么，而不影响模型对其他说话者（用户）的预测。作者要检验这条"边界"是否成立，以判断后训练究竟只是安装表层的助手人设，还是更深地改变了模型的内部表征。

Method: 检验助手"偏好无害而非有害任务"这一安全相关偏好在用户轮上的表现：对比预训练基座模型与后训练模型；在多个开放权重模型家族中复现；考察随模型规模的变化；并用不涉及用户轮的狭窄微调检验该偏好是否可被移动。

Result: 该偏好在预训练基座模型中很小或接近零，经后训练后出现，在多个开放权重模型家族中复现，随规模增大而增强，并且可被从未接触用户轮的狭窄微调所改变——说明偏好确实影响用户轮上的预测。

Conclusion: 后训练不只是安装一个浅层的助手人设，而是将其泛化到助手发言轮之外，渗入模型对用户（即用户所说内容）的表征之中。

Abstract: Post-training turns a general next-token predictor into a chat model with a persistent assistant persona. If that persona is a character the model plays only on its own turns, its preferences should govern what the assistant says, not what the model predicts other speakers will say. We test this boundary and find that it does not hold: a safety-relevant preference of the assistant---for harmless over harmful tasks---shapes the model's predictions even on the user's turn, where the assistant is not the one speaking. We find that this preference is small or near-zero in pretrained base models, that it emerges through post-training, replicated across open-weight model families, grows with scale, and can be moved by narrow finetuning that never touches user turns. We claim that this is evidence that post-training does not merely install a shallow assistant persona, but instead generalises beyond just the local assistant turn, into the model's representation of the user.

</details>


### [354] [XYEval: Agents say yes to bad advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23939)
*Zhengxuan Wu,Yuxuan Li,Oyvind Tafjord,Been Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出XYEval框架，将现有基准转化为XY问题评估，发现当前AI智能体在面对用户误导性建议时性能大幅下降（最高达46.7%），简单系统指令仅能部分缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户与AI智能体的有效沟通是人机协作的关键。XY问题是一种常见沟通陷阱：用户询问的是其尝试的解决方案而非真正的问题。先前关于谄媚（sycophancy）的评估尚未扩展到智能体场景下的XY问题，而智能体是否能抵抗看似合理但具误导性的用户建议并清晰传达推理，仍缺乏系统评估。

Method: 提出XYEval——一种元评估框架，可将现有基准转化为XY问题评估。在六个多样化基准套件上评估五个模型，并引入τ²-bench测试遇到要求详细解释才批准更优方案的挑剔用户时的表现，同时进行大量执行轨迹分析。

Result: 在XY变异下，各基准上智能体出现大幅XY性能下降，相对降幅最高达46.7%。在τ²-bench中，面对挑剔用户时性能下降更明显。鼓励关注XY问题的简单系统指令基线仅提供部分缓解。

Conclusion: 当前智能体在面对误导性建议时缺乏有效推理与沟通能力。缓解XY问题仍然困难，需要智能体既能识别用户的误导，又能清晰传达真正的问题。

Abstract: Effective communication between users and AI agents is essential for human-AI collaboration. The XY problem is a well-known communication pitfall where a person asks about their attempted solution rather than their actual problem. We extend prior sycophancy evaluation to the XY problem in agentic settings, evaluating whether agents can resist plausible but misleading suggestions from users and communicate their reasoning. We introduce XYEval, a meta-evaluation framework that can transform an existing benchmark into an XY problem evaluation. We evaluate five models across six diverse benchmark suites. Agents suffer large XY drops under XY mutation across benchmarks, with relative drops reaching up to 46.7%. With $τ^2$-bench, we further show that agent performance drops more when encountering a pedantic user who requires detailed explanations before approving a better solution. Our findings suggest that current agents lack the ability to effectively reason and communicate when facing misleading suggestions. A simple system instruction baseline that encourages awareness of XY problems only offers partial mitigation. Extensive trace analyses provide behavioral insights into how and why these XY drops occur across execution trajectories. Our results show that mitigating the XY problem remains challenging, requiring agents to both recognize user misdirection and clearly communicate the underlying problem.

</details>


### [355] [HaikuS2S: A Cascaded System For Responding In Verse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23951)
*Devangi Sharma,Sophia Judicke,Glenda Tan,Conrad Schaumburg,Shinji Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HaikuS2S级联系统，结合ASR、LLM生成的俳句和TTS微调，用于生成符合俳句5-7-5音节结构和行末停顿的诗歌语音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有韵律迁移和微调诗歌TTS系统虽能提升表现力并捕捉诗歌语调，但未能建模俳句特有的5-7-5音节结构及行末停顿。

Method: 构建级联系统HaikuS2S：包括ASR、LLM生成俳句，以及在散文和自定义俳句数据集上进行TTS微调；评估聚焦情感相似度、语音质量和韵律对齐。

Result: 微调系统显著改善了韵律和声调对齐，尤其是同时在通用诗歌和俳句上训练的系统；各系统间情感相似度得分保持相近。

Conclusion: 在通用诗歌与俳句数据上联合微调TTS，可在不牺牲情感相似度的前提下，有效提升俳句语音的结构化韵律与声调对齐。

Abstract: Expressive speech synthesis has advanced through prosody modeling, yet generating structured poetic speech, such as haiku, remains challenging. Prior work on prosody transfer improves expressiveness, and fine-tuned poetry TTS (text-to-speech) systems capture verse intonation. However, these models do not model haiku's 5-7-5 syllable structure or line-ending pauses. We present a cascaded system, HaikuS2S, combining ASR (automatic speech recognition), LLM (large language model)-generated haiku, and TTS fine-tuning on both prose and custom haiku datasets. Our evaluation focuses on emotion similarity, speech quality, and prosody alignment. In our experiments, we see that our prosody and tonal alignment improve significantly with our fine-tuned systems, particularly the one trained on both general poetry and haiku. We also see that we maintain similar emotion similarity scores across all systems.

</details>


### [356] [Some Dialects Are More Equal Than Others: Non-Prestigious Arabic Dialectal Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23955)
*Mai Mohamed Eida,Ryan Dolan,Paul de Nijs,Jonathan Dunn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究考察LLM对埃及阿拉伯语方言的偏见：Sa'idi方言（SEA）在NLP中代表性不足。通过定向句法评估发现多个LLM对SEA存在显著偏见，且该偏见导致MMLU任务性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往埃及阿拉伯语NLP研究多聚焦于地位较高的开罗方言（CEA），导致地位较低的Sa'idi方言（SEA）在LLM和资源开发中缺乏代表性。作者想探究这种代表性缺失是否影响LLM对SEA可接受性的判断（上游），以及上游偏见是否导致下游任务表现变差。

Method: 采用定向句法评估任务考察SEA方言特征对LLM偏好的上游影响；随后分析这些相同特征对MMLU基准下游模型性能的影响。

Result: 定向句法评估揭示多个LLM对SEA存在显著偏见；在下游MMLU基准上，模型在呈现SEA时性能出现退化。

Conclusion: 上游方言偏见会传导至下游任务性能下降，凸显了需进一步研究次方言变异如何影响语言技术的必要性。

Abstract: Previous work on Egyptian Arabic in NLP has focused largely on the prestigious Cairene Egyptian Arabic (CEA) dialect, resulting in a lack of representation for the less prestigious Sa'idi Egyptian Arabic (SEA) dialect both in LLM and resource development. Does this lack of representation influence an LLM's view of the acceptability of SEA (upstream), and does an upstream bias against SEA lead to worse performance (downstream)? We investigate the upstream effect of SEA dialectal features on LLM preferences in a Targeted Syntactic Evaluation (TSE) task which reveals a significant bias against SEA across multiple LLMs. We then analyze the effect of these same features on downstream model performance on MMLU benchmarks and show that models experience a degradation in performance when presented with SEA. This work highlights the need for further exploration on how sub-dialectal variation impacts language technologies.

</details>


### [357] [Open-Jev Judgments on CallScreenBench: Calibrated One-Pass Scam Screening with a Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23959)
*Simiao Ren,Kidus Zewde,Xingyu Shen,Yuchen Zhou,Dennis Ng,Ankit Raj,Tommy Duong,Yuxin Zhang,Neo Tiangratanakul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出JevLite读出头方案（Qwen3-4B经LoRA微调，对两个答案标签logits做温度标定softmax输出P(scam)），在合成诈骗电话筛查任务上实现毫秒级、单次前向的概率决策，AUROC .974、校准误差 .052，性能不劣于LLM裁判且更快更早。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诈骗电话筛查需要在每一轮通话后以毫秒级速度给出可信的概率判断，而生成式LLM回答存在延迟高、无校准概率的问题；Jev式类型化决策（输入声明选项，单次前向输出每个选项的校准概率，不生成文本）理论上匹配该需求，因此需要实证其在电话诈骗筛查上的表现。

Method: 采用JevLite这一开放实现：对Qwen3-4B进行LoRA微调，使两个答案标签logits经温度缩放softmax后给出P(scam)；在41个留出的CallScreenBench场景（577个逐轮决策）上以三种子集成评估，并与LLM裁判（MiniMax-M3）在预设.02边际下做非劣性比较，同时对照微调的ModernBERT编码器以及同一骨干的生成式微调版本。

Result: 三种子集成AUROC .974、校准误差 .052，在预设.02边际下非劣于LLM裁判；对合法通话零误报；在同一挂断规则下决策提前1.14轮；单张消费级GPU每次决策64.5 ms，比同一骨干的生成式微调快4.9倍；微调的ModernBERT编码器性能没有显著更差。

Conclusion: 性能提升主要来自读出方式与校准而非准确率本身；该方案无架构创新，贡献在于应用与一套同时报告校准、误报和决策时机的评估；且配方选择过程使用了测试集、所有来电者均为合成数据，结论存在局限。

Abstract: Screening a phone call for fraud needs a trustworthy probability after every caller turn, in milliseconds. Jev-style typed decisions promise exactly that: declared options go in, one calibrated probability per option comes out of a single forward pass, with no generated text. We test an open implementation of this readout, JevLite, on scam-call screening: Qwen3-4B is LoRA-tuned so that the temperature-scaled softmax over two answer-label logits is P(scam). On 41 held-out CallScreenBench scenarios (577 per-turn decisions) a three-seed ensemble reaches AUROC .974 with calibration error .052, non-inferior to an LLM judge (MiniMax-M3) at a pre-registered .02 margin, with no false alarms on legitimate calls, decisions 1.14 turns earlier under the same hang-up rule, and 64.5 ms per decision on one consumer GPU, 4.9x lower than the same backbone fine-tuned to generate its answer. The gain is in the readout and calibration, not accuracy: a fine-tuned ModernBERT encoder is not significantly worse, the recipe was selected with test-set exposure, and all callers are synthetic. We claim no architectural novelty; the contribution is the application and an evaluation reporting calibration, false alarms and decision timing alongside AUROC.

</details>


### [358] [From Tables to Quantified Statements: Evaluating LLM Inference Generation through Executable Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23966)
*Mai Mohamed Eida,Gunjan Anand,Ayush Singh,Aleksandre Maskharashvili*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STAT-TO-TEXT任务，让LLM从统计表生成含量化词的自然语言推断，并用LLM生成的Python检查器验证真值条件，比较四个开源模型的忠实性、逻辑准确性、覆盖度与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能从表格生成流畅描述，但输出可能缺乏结构化数据的逻辑支撑。现有评估难以检验生成内容在逻辑上是否被表格数据支持，因此需要一个可控的量化推断任务来考察忠实性与逻辑准确性。

Method: 构建STAT-TO-TEXT受控任务，要求LLM使用all、some、no、most等量化结构，从统计表生成量化自然语言推断；采用LLM生成的Python检查器代码，通过执行代码将推断的真值条件与表格对照验证；比较四个不同家族与规模的开源权重LLM，评估忠实性、逻辑准确性、表格覆盖度与多样性，并进行人工标注验证。

Result: 模型规模与家族均有影响；最大的模型GPT-OSS-120B始终产生最忠实的推断，且未牺牲表格覆盖度与量化词多样性，优于较小模型。人工标注显示自动检查器与人类判断高度一致。

Conclusion: STAT-TO-TEXT为评估LLM从表格生成量化推断的忠实性与逻辑性提供了可控框架，基于代码执行的自动检查器与人工判断高度吻合，模型规模与家族显著影响表现。

Abstract: LLMs can generate fluent descriptions from tables, but their outputs may remain logically unsupported by the structured data. We introduce STAT-TO-TEXT, a controlled task in which LLMs generate quantified natural language inferences from statistical tables using quantified constructions such as all, some, no, and most. To evaluate these inferences, we use an LLM generated Python checker code which when executed verifies the corresponding truth conditions against the table. We compare four open-weight LLMs across model families and scales, evaluating faithfulness, logical accuracy, table coverage, and diversity. Our results show that model scale and family matter, with the largest model (GPT-OSS-120B) consistently producing the most faithful inferences without sacrificing greater table coverage and quantifier diversity, as opposed to smaller models. These findings are supported by human annotation, which shows that the automated checker closely aligns with human judgments.

</details>


### [359] [When Evidence Conflicts: Reliability-aware Meta-review Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24028)
*Xinzhe Wang,Fei Tao,Jiang Xie,Hong Yu,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出可靠性感知的证据聚合框架用于元综述生成，通过提取面向方面的意见、识别冲突证据、估计意见支持度与评审质量，并对评审反馈加权，使生成器优先采纳支持充分的观点，在自动与人工评测中均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元综述生成多被建模为多文档摘要并统一聚合评审意见，当评审证据相互冲突且可靠性不一时，难以判断应优先采纳哪些观点。因此需要引入可靠性感知的证据聚合来解决分歧下的意见优先级问题。

Method: 框架首先从同行评审中提取方面级意见，并在每个方面内识别冲突证据；随后估计意见级支持度与评审级质量以衡量证据可靠性；基于这些信号为评审反馈分配可靠性感知权重，使生成器优先采用支持更充分的论点，同时保留多样视角。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在自动评测与人工评测中均持续优于强基线，尤其在高冲突评审场景下，在冲突识别与冲突解决方面提升明显。

Conclusion: 通过可靠性感知的证据聚合可有效提升元综述生成质量，冲突场景下的收益尤为突出；代码与实现细节已公开。

Abstract: Generating coherent meta-reviews from multiple peer reviews is challenging when reviewer evidence conflicts and varies in reliability. Existing approaches typically formulate meta-review generation as a multi-document summarization task and aggregate reviewer feedback uniformly, making it difficult to determine which opinions should be prioritized under disagreement. In this paper, we study meta-review generation through reliability-aware evidence aggregation. Our framework first extracts aspect-level opinions from peer reviews and identifies conflicting evidence within each aspect. It then estimates opinion-level support and review-level quality to measure evidence reliability. Based on these signals, the framework assigns reliability-aware weights to reviewer feedback, enabling the generator to prioritize better-supported arguments while preserving diverse perspectives. Experiments demonstrate that our method consistently improves meta-review generation over strong baselines on both automatic and human evaluations, with clear gains in conflict recognition and resolution under high-conflict review scenarios. The code and implementation details are publicly available at https://github.com/Wangxz729/reliability-aware-meta-review.

</details>


### [360] [Calibrated Decisions at Scale: Converting Police Crash Narratives into Probabilistic Crash Variables with a System One Model (Jev)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24052)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将交通事故叙事编码构建为"有门控的类型化决策"任务，采用只输出概率、不生成文本的System One模型Jev，对德州499,500条叙事进行筛查、195,857条按27问模式编码；结果与人工标注对比F1达0.908，并通过校准审计、分辨率下限界定和复核预算，展示了叙事信息对编码字段的增量价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有事故数据集中的调查叙事蕴含编码字段所遗漏的信息，但大规模编码面临三大障碍：前沿大语言模型在此规模下成本高昂、其生成文本无法验证、且缺乏规定人工需核查多少输出的规则。本文旨在建立一个可审计、可控制成本与人工复核量的叙事编码框架。

Method: 将叙事编码形式化为有门控的类型化决策，由System One模型Jev回答，输出分析师定义选项上的概率且不生成文本。用筛查流程覆盖499,500条德州叙事，其中195,857条按27问模式编码。成本由模式规模而非叙事长度决定。概率审计对照编码字段及在明确抽样设计下获得的2,416条盲化人工判断，并对两个前沿大语言模型在同一记录上做基准比较；另提出分辨率下限界与基于标记记录的复核预算。

Result: 相对人工标签，类型化模型F1为0.908；一个前沿模型高出0.059，另一个与其无显著差异。校准因模型而异而非因范式而异，故每个模型都需审计；在同一标签上重校准使校准误差降低3.3倍。与编码字段的一致度中位数低估了0.26个kappa的叙事保真度。将校准后的变量加入编码字段后，归因于九个因素的伤害与致死事故每年增加10,747起。

Conclusion: 不同架构模型均需单独审计，范式并不决定校准水平；只输出概率的编码方式可验证、成本可控并支持明确的复核预算。叙事变量能揭示编码字段遗漏的事故信息，显著补充传统编码数据。

Abstract: Crash datasets that carry an investigator narrative hold information the coded fields omit. Coding those narratives at scale has been blocked by three obstacles. Frontier large language models are costly at that scale, their generated text cannot be verified, and no rule says how much output a human must check. This paper formulates narrative coding as gated, typed decisions answered by Jev, a System One model that returns probabilities over analyst-defined options and generates no text. A screen covered 499,500 Texas narratives and 195,857 were coded with a 27-question schema. Cost is governed by schema size rather than narrative length. The probabilities are audited against coded fields and against 2,416 blinded human judgments drawn under a stated sampling design. Two frontier large language models are benchmarked on the same records. Against human labels the typed model attains an F1 of 0.908. One frontier model gains 0.059 and the other is indistinguishable from it. Calibration varies by model rather than by paradigm, so each model must be audited. Recalibration on the same labels reduces calibration error by a factor of 3.3. Agreement with coded fields understates fidelity to the narrative by a median of 0.26 in kappa. A resolution-floor bound covers any model that reports probabilities on a discrete grid. A review budget over flagged records gives the records a human must read per variable and per year. Adding the calibrated variables to the coded fields raises the injury and fatal crashes attributed to nine factors by 10,747 per year.

</details>


### [361] [Efficient Reasoning Exploration via State-Conditioned Latent Steering with Progress Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24066)
*Hengyuan Zhang,Chenming Shang,Zunhai Su,Xiao Liang,Hui Shen,Jing Xiong,Dawei Li,Shiping Yang,Kailai Yang,Wei Zhang,Ruobing Xie,Hayden Kwok-Hay So,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPS（State-conditioned Progress-guided Steering），一种免训练的隐空间引导框架，通过状态条件下的方向库在高不确定性推理转折点施加进度导向的引导向量，缓解推理模型在Best-of-N中的探索崩溃问题，在多种模型规模和基准上持续优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N等推理策略的效果依赖于采样候选能否覆盖多样且高质量的推理路径。然而经过后训练的推理模型常出现"探索崩溃"：独立采样反复沿相似推理路径，增加采样预算收益有限。现有方法虽鼓励更广探索，但未显式引导探索朝向有实质进展的延续方向，导致探索效率受限。

Method: 提出SPS（State-conditioned Progress-guided Steering），一种免训练的隐空间引导框架。首先构建状态条件下的方向库（Direction Bank），为不同前缀状态区域存储多个进度导向的引导向量；在线推理时，根据当前前缀状态检索合适的引导向量，并在高不确定性转折点施加该向量，引导下一步推理走向有意义的进展。

Result: 在多种模型规模和多个基准上的大量实验表明，SPS持续优于强基线；进一步分析验证了各关键设计的有效性，并为未来研究提供了有价值的启示。

Conclusion: SPS通过状态条件检索与进度导向引导的结合，有效缓解探索崩溃、提升推理探索效率，且无需额外训练即可推广；代码已开源。

Abstract: Best-of-$N$ is a widely used inference strategy for complex reasoning, whose effectiveness depends on whether sampled candidates can cover diverse and high-quality reasoning paths. However, post-trained reasoning models often suffer from \emph{exploration collapse}, where independent rollouts repeatedly follow similar reasoning paths and limit the gains from increasing the rollout budget. Existing methods alleviate this issue by promoting broader exploration, but do not explicitly guide exploration toward continuations that make meaningful progress, resulting in limited exploration efficiency. To address this, we propose \emph{\underline{S}tate-conditioned \underline{P}rogress-guided \underline{S}teering} (SPS), a training-free latent steering framework. Specifically, SPS constructs a state-conditioned Direction Bank containing multiple progress-guided steering vectors for different prefix-state regions. During online inference, SPS retrieves a suitable steering vector based on the current prefix state and applies it at high-uncertainty transitions to guide the next reasoning step toward meaningful progress. Extensive experiments across multiple model scales and benchmarks demonstrate that SPS consistently outperforms strong baselines. Further analyses validate the effectiveness of its key designs and offer valuable insights for future research. The code is available at https://github.com/rattlesnakey/SPS.

</details>


### [362] [From Content Generation to Learning Support: Pedagogy-Guided Generative Video Tutors for STEM Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24083)
*Xinchen Ma,Shuimu Wang,Gaole He,Yanbin Zhang,Chunyang Wang,Yunshi Lan,Weining Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PIVOT框架，将教学法贯穿生成式教学视频全流程，在四个STEM领域实现教学对齐、质量可控并支持评估与纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI教学视频系统主要追求视觉连贯的成品，而非支持学习。生成视频普遍缺乏显性教学结构、可靠的质量控制，以及评估学习者理解、纠正错误概念的机制，因此难以真正服务教学。

Method: PIVOT（Pedagogy-guided Instructional VideO Tutoring）受传统教学工作流启发，将教学法融入整个生成管线：先用教学原则指导分镜脚本生成；再通过代码为中心的生成方式与教学验证机制，产出经校验的多模态视频；最后将视频与评估环节、错误概念感知的补救教学相衔接。

Result: 在四个STEM领域开展的实验与专家评估表明，该框架生成的教学视频内容与教学法对齐，呈现清晰且具吸引力，教学流程连贯，并被感知为对学习有效。

Conclusion: 研究主张以人为中心的教育内容生成视角：生成系统不仅应以其产出物来评价和设计，还应考察其如何支持教学实践、学习者理解与矫正性反馈。

Abstract: Generative AI enables scalable production of educational videos, but current systems largely focus on producing visually coherent content rather than supporting learning. As a result, generated videos often lack explicit pedagogical structure, reliable quality control, and mechanisms for assessing learner understanding or addressing misconceptions. In this work, we introduce PIVOT (Pedagogy-guided Instructional VideO Tutoring), a generative video tutoring framework for STEM learning via learning-centered instructional support.1 Inspired by conventional teaching workflows, our framework integrates pedagogy into the full generation pipeline: it first uses instructional principles to guide storyboard generation, then produces verified multimodal videos through code-centric generation and a pedagogical verification harness, and finally connects videos with assessment and misconception-aware remediation. Experiments and expert evaluations across four STEM domains show that our framework produces educational videos with pedagogically aligned content, clear and engaging presentation, coherent instructional flow, and perceived effectiveness for learning. These findings suggest a human-centered perspective on educational content generation: generative systems should be evaluated and designed not only by what they produce, but also by how they support teaching practices, learner understanding, and corrective feedback.

</details>


### [363] [You Can Tell Who's Asking: What the Web's Questions Are Made Of, and Where They Come From](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24106)
*Calvin Zhou,Vincent McCloskey,Krishna Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对网络问题的来源进行大规模历时测量，发现多数问题为模板化/制造内容，问题频率无法反映真实需求，且真实用户提问占比在12年间大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界和工业界（QA训练数据、检索基准、内容策略）普遍假设网页上的问题能反映人的真实意图，但这一假设从未在大规模数据上得到验证。作者希望检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 从110个FineWeb快照（2013-2025）中抽取134亿条问题出现记录，通过逻辑回归模型，利用问题长度及上下文（而非问题类型）区分真实用户提问与模板化/制造问题，并分析问题频率、来源分布及随时间的演变。

Result: 模型区分真实与制造问题的AUC为0.725，但针对商业FAQ写作仅0.554；最高频问题中超70%为模板/样板；12年间真实问题占比下降79%（控制抓取构成后仍为42-56%），且问题长度和上下文呈下降趋势。

Conclusion: 问题出现次数衡量的是字符串被发布的频率而非被提问的频率；可抓取网络中的问题已从人类提问转向为机器阅读而制造，基于网页问题代理人类意图的假设不成立。

Abstract: Questions scraped from the web are used across academia and industry as a proxy for what people want to know. Across QA training data, retrieval benchmarks, and content strategy, questions on a page are assumed to reflect human intent. We test this assumption at scale by extracting 13.4B question occurrences across 110 FineWeb snapshots (2013-2025), and report three findings. First, you can tell who is asking: provenance (the host/page of questions) leaves a signal in question form, and a logistic model can separate genuine user questions from templated/manufactured ones at AUC 0.725 via length and surrounding context rather than question type, though only 0.554 against commerce FAQ writing. Second, question frequency does not measure demand: the most-frequent questions are boilerplate/templated (over 70% of the top thousand), so occurrence counts measure how often a string was published and not how often it was asked. Third, over twelve years the genuine share of occurrences fell by 79% (42-56% after controlling for crawl composition), with question length and context decreasing. We present the first diachronic, occurrence-level measurement of web question provenance, and find the crawlable web's questions have shifted from being asked by humans toward manufactured for machines to read.

</details>


### [364] [Re:CAP - Auditing Retrieval Coverage in Production RAG Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24122)
*Aviral Joshi,Hanoz Bhathena,Max Nelson,Saket Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Re:CAP，一种无参考的检索覆盖审计方法，通过迭代探测缺失文档而非穷举相关文档，用于生产环境中RAG检索质量的审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RAG难以监控：面向实时重建索引的非平稳百万级文档语料库不存在穷尽的相关性标注，导致检索质量研究不足，且常被以生成为中心的指标所忽视。

Method: Re:CAP是一个应用于已部署RAG流水线的初始答案与检索上下文的参考无关审计循环：识别已覆盖主题，为可能缺失的主题生成探测性问题，检索候选文档，并用LLM-as-judge仅保留带来未检索过信息的文档。

Result: 在四个公开基准上，Re:CAP恢复平铺BM25 top-500无法触及的9-29%金标注，在TREC-COVID上达48%；在MuSiQue上以不到一半文档预算，recall比平铺混合top-500高+12.9个百分点。BM25、稠密、混合集成top-500在TREC-COVID仍漏掉21.2%金文档，Re:CAP可恢复其中78.9%被人工判定为新信息（Fleiss κ=0.79, n=123），生产流量上为73.9%（n=180）。端到端recall在三次独立运行中可复现至±1%。

Conclusion: Re:CAP能稳定恢复被基线检索遗漏的文档并引入新信息，端到端recall高度可复现，可作为周期性检索审计的稳定工具。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is hard to monitor in production: exhaustive relevance labels do not exist for non-stationary multi-million-passage corpora that re-index in real time. As a result, retrieval quality is generally understudied and often deprioritised in favour of generation-oriented metrics. In this work, we propose auditing retrieval coverage by probing for evidence of missing documents rather than enumerating every relevant one. Our method Re:CAP (REtrieval Coverage Audit by iterative Probing) is a reference-free audit loop applied to a deployed RAG pipeline's initial answer and retrieved context: it identifies the topics already covered, generates probing questions for plausibly missing topics, retrieves candidate documents, and applies an LLM-as-judge to retain only those that introduce previously-unretrieved information. On four public benchmarks, Re:CAP recovers 9-29% of gold labels that flat BM25 top-500 cannot reach, rising to 48% on TREC-COVID. On MuSiQue Re:CAP beats flat hybrid top-500 by +12.9 pp on recall at less than half the document budget. An ensemble BM25, dense, and hybrid baseline (top-500 each) still leaves out 21.2% of gold docs on TREC-COVID that Re:CAP recovers; human annotators judge that 78.9% of those structurally distinct documents add new information to the baseline answer (Fleiss $κ$ = 0.79, n = 123), and 73.9% on live production traffic (n = 180). End-to-end recall is reproducible to within $\pm$1% across three independent runs, making Re:CAP a stable instrument for periodic retrieval audits.

</details>


### [365] [TAC-Time: Texts as Channels For Multimodal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24156)
*Jiayi Liang,Xiaotian Gu,Xinyu Xie,Yuanbin Wu,Xiaoling Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TAC-Time框架，将文本信息转化为额外的时间通道，与数值序列在共享时序主干中联合建模，兼顾时序连续性与效率，并在多模态预测基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序预测方法多仅依赖数值观测，忽略辅助文本中的丰富上下文信息；而现有多模态方法要么把文本当作静态特征，要么用大语言模型作为预测主干，因而难以捕捉时序动态并带来较高计算成本。

Method: TAC-Time将文本特征转换为额外的时间通道，与数值序列在共享的时序主干网络中进行联合建模，从而保留时间连续性与周期结构，同时保持高效与可扩展性；该建模方式还支持系统性的可解释性分析（如注意力与频域分析）。

Result: 通过注意力与频域分析展示了强跨模态依赖关系，识别出具有预测性的文本信号，且相关性对齐可带来部分预测性能提升；在真实多模态基准上的大量实验表明TAC-Time优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 将文本转化为额外时间通道并与数值序列联合建模，是一种高效、可扩展且可解释的多模态时序预测方案。

Abstract: Most existing time series forecasting methods rely solely on numerical observations, overlooking rich contextual information from auxiliary texts. Recent multimodal approaches attempt to incorporate textual signals, but they often treat text as static features or use large language models as forecasting backbones, limiting their ability to capture temporal dynamics and increasing computational cost. To address these challenges, we propose TAC-Time, a unified framework that transforms textual information into additional temporal channels. By modeling text features jointly with numerical sequences in a shared temporal backbone, TAC-Time preserves temporal continuity and periodic structures while remaining efficient and scalable. This formulation also enables systematic interpretability analyses. We show strong cross-modal dependencies through attention and frequency-domain analyses, and identify predictive textual signals whose correlation-aware alignment yields partial forecasting improvements. Extensive experiments on real-world multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that TAC-Time outperforms prior methods.

</details>


### [366] [Efficient LLM Distillation for Bangladesh Legal Context: A Smartphone-Compatible Retrieval-Augmented Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24177)
*MD. Nafis Kamal,Mahadi Hasan Fahim,Talha Ridwan,Nadifa Zaman,Fariha Roushon Florin,Farig Yousuf Sadeque,Saadat Rafid Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过两阶段渐进式知识蒸馏，将90亿参数的Gemma-2压缩为20亿参数学生模型，并配合混合检索，实现孟加拉国成文法离线问答，在Pixel 6上以1.6 GB量化模型运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国法律信息对多数公民不可及：法条仅英文、律师集中于城市、依赖云端的AI在移动网络不稳时失效，且法律文本幻觉会造成直接危害。已有法律语言模型多面向通用法律英语，缺少针对孟加拉国成文法的专用系统。

Method: 两阶段渐进式知识蒸馏：第一阶段用9,429条质量筛选后的法律问答对（从14,514条生成查询中65%获采纳）做监督微调；第二阶段在温度τ=4.0下对教师模型每token前50个logits最小化稀疏KL散度，通过QLoRA（4-bit NF4、rank-32 LoRA适配器）实现。所有回答经混合检索接地，融合稠密语义搜索（60%）与BM25（40%），覆盖宪法及国家立法的36,029个法条段落。仅处理成文法解释，不含判例推理。

Result: 50条英语基准上ROUGE-L 0.4715、BERTScore F1 0.5679，相较未蒸馏的检索增强基线（0.2323、0.2340）分别提升103%和143%；适配器量化为1.6 GB（GGUF Q4_K_M），Pixel 6无网络下4-8 tokens/秒；50条孟加拉语查询跨语言评测ROUGE-L 0.4083、BERTScore 0.8133，可从孟加拉语输入检索英文语料；单评估者试点中执业律师对50条回答加权均分4.16/5（90%为4或5分）。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏加混合检索可在低资源、离线移动场景下有效提供孟加拉国成文法问答服务，跨语言检索表现良好，律师评分显示其实用性超出文本重叠指标，但评测规模有限且不覆盖判例推理。

Abstract: Legal information in Bangladesh is inaccessible to most citizens. Statutory text is English-only, trained lawyers are concentrated in urban centres, and cloud-dependent AI fails where mobile connectivity is unreliable, a setting in which hallucinated legal text causes direct harm. The system addresses statutory interpretation only; queries that require judicial precedent or case-law reasoning fall outside its scope. We target the statutory access gap by compressing a 9-billion-parameter Gemma-2 teacher into a 2-billion-parameter student through two-phase progressive knowledge distillation. Phase 1 performs supervised fine-tuning on 9,429 quality-gated legal question-answer pairs (65% acceptance from 14,514 generated queries); Phase 2 minimises sparse Kullback-Leibler divergence against the teacher's top-50 per-token logits at temperature tau = 4.0, implemented via QLoRA (4-bit NF4, rank-32 LoRA adapters). Prior legal language models target general legal English; this system specialises in Bangladeshi statutory law. Every response is grounded through hybrid retrieval combining dense semantic search (60%) and BM25 (40%) across 36,029 statutory passages from the Bangladesh Constitution and national legislation. On a 50-query English benchmark, the distilled model reaches ROUGE-L 0.4715 and BERTScore F1 0.5679, a 103% ROUGE-L and 143% BERTScore gain over the retrieval-augmented undistilled baseline (ROUGE-L 0.2323, BERTScore 0.2340). The adapter quantises to 1.6 GB (GGUF Q4_K_M) and runs at 4-8 tokens per second on a Pixel 6 with no network access. Cross-lingual evaluation on 50 Bangla queries yields ROUGE-L 0.4083 and BERTScore 0.8133, showing effective retrieval from Bangla input against an English-only corpus. In a single-evaluator pilot, a practising lawyer rated 50 responses at a weighted mean of 4.16/5 (90% rated 4 or 5), supporting utility beyond text-overlap metrics.

</details>


### [367] [When Residualization Helps an Audit: Format Effects, Slice Gains, and Their Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24194)
*Daein Weon,Dongho Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，用于LLM系统的评分（奖励模型、重排器、LLM评判）常追踪表面形式而非所声称的质量；简单地"残差化"去除表面成分并不能得到更有效的测量，因为被去除的成分可能携带构念相关信息。作者通过设计干预和观察性实验证明残差化会削弱格式效应但同时损害构念对齐，并提出一个报告协议：调整后的分数只能作为审计期诊断报告，不可替代原始分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型、重排器和LLM评判等评分系统常受到表面形式（如注释、格式）的干扰，导致评分追踪的是表面特征而非真实的代码/文本质量。实践中流行的做法是减去可预测的表面成分（残差化），但作者质疑这一做法：被去除的成分可能包含与构念相关的信号，而残差化本身无法区分哪些是噪声、哪些是有用信号。

Method: 1) 使用MBPP问题，构造"简洁正确解"与"带注释的错误解"配对，测试公开偏好奖励模型的选择能力；2) 设计干预实验（仅修改注释、保持单元测试标签不变），量化残差化对格式效应和正确vs错误边际的影响；3) 在观察性NLI和QA设置中，预先冻结留出复制集，用不重叠标注者的标签重新评估；4) 分析构念与表面特征纠缠时的残差化后果，并用受控模型检验预调整检查是否能保证安全。

Result: 公开偏好奖励模型在正确解与注释错误解之间的选择准确率仅为0.507，接近抛硬币水平。设计干预下，残差化将格式效应削弱约0.12（无论代码正确还是错误），但正确vs错误边际变动小于0.01。在观察性NLI和QA设置中，只有在预先声明的"表面预测器出错"的切片上，构念标签一致性才有所改善；但全群体一致性在所有报告有正切片收益的观察性设置中都下降，且在所有此类QA设置中问题内排序也下降。受控模型显示，对构念对齐同样有害的配置也能通过所有预调整检查。

Conclusion: 残差化可以在去相关的同时降低构念对齐，且不存在可靠的预调整门控保证。作者提出一个报告协议：调整后分数的结论（包括拒绝给出结论）必须说明其可声称的内容——调整分数只能作为审计期诊断，与其带来的构念对齐代价一同报告，绝不能替代原始分数。

Abstract: Evaluation scores used around LLM systems -- including reward models, rerankers, and LLM judges -- can track surface form instead of the quality they claim to measure. When presented with a terse correct solution and a commented buggy solution for the same MBPP problem, a public preference reward model selects the correct one no better than a coin flip (0.507). Subtracting the predictable surface component from such scores is increasingly common, but removal alone does not yield a more valid measurement: the removed component may carry construct-relevant signal, and residualization cannot tell which is which. Under designed interventions -- unit-test labels with comment-only edits -- residualization attenuates the reward model's format effects by about 0.12 on both correct and buggy code, while the correct-versus-buggy margins move by less than 0.01. In observational NLI and QA settings, we freeze a held-out replication before scoring and re-evaluate it using labels from disjoint annotators; this supports only a narrower conclusion: better agreement with the construct labels on a pre-declared slice where a surface-only predictor errs, not a repaired score. Full-population agreement falls in every observational setting with a reported positive slice gain, and within-question ranking falls in every such QA setting. When construct and surface features are entangled, residualization can decorrelate a score while degrading construct alignment, and, in a controlled model, configurations just as damaging to construct alignment pass every pre-adjustment check, so no committed gate is a guarantee. We assemble these distinctions into a reporting protocol whose outcomes, refusal included, state what an adjusted score may be claimed to show: an audit-time diagnostic reported beside the construct-alignment cost it incurs, never a replacement for the raw score.

</details>


### [368] [LoopCD: Loop-wise Contrastive Decoding for Improving Reasoning in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24196)
*Byeongho Yu,Junhyuk So,Eunhyeok Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 LoopCD，一种无需训练的循环式对比解码方法，通过对比早期迭代与最终迭代的 logits 来修正 LoopLM 中的不确定"硬"token，提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型（LoopLM）通过共享权重递归精炼隐表示进行"潜在推理"，优于显式语言推理，但存在循环不稳定性：迭代精炼过程不稳定会产生局部的、不确定的"硬"token，并伴随推理错误。

Method: LoopCD（loop-wise contrastive decoding）在推理阶段干预这些硬 token。利用模型内部动态，将较早迭代的 logits 与最后一次精炼迭代的 logits 进行对比，形成最终的采样分布。

Result: 该方法效率极高，仅引入可忽略的推理开销，且无需额外训练，能自然精炼推理关键的硬 token，从而有效提升推理表现。大量实验表明，该方法在多种推理任务上提升了近期代表性 LoopLM 的性能。

Conclusion: 通过循环间对比解码可在推理时缓解 LoopLM 的循环不稳定性，无需训练即提升推理效果，为潜在推理模型的推理增强提供了轻量高效的方案。

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) perform "latent reasoning" by recursively refining internal latent representations with shared weights, offering a more effective alternative to explicit verbal reasoning. Despite their effectiveness, we find that LoopLMs remain prone to loop instability: unstable refinement across iterations can produce localized uncertain "hard" tokens associated with reasoning errors. To address this, we propose LoopCD, loop-wise contrastive decoding that enhances the reasoning performance of LoopLMs by intervening on these tokens at inference time. Specifically, we exploit the internal dynamics of LoopLMs and contrast the logits from earlier iterations with logits from the last refined iteration to form the final sampling distribution. We find that this strategy is highly efficient, introducing only negligible inference overhead and requiring no additional training, while effectively improving reasoning performance by naturally refining reasoning-critical hard tokens. Extensive experiments show that our method improves the performance of recent representative LoopLMs across various reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [369] [Vimarsha: Faithful ASR Evaluation for Indian Languages with Demographic Diversity, In-the-Wild Audio and Spelling Variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24199)
*Kaushal Santosh Bhogale,Srija Anand,Sadakopa Ramakrishnan Thothathiri,Tahir Javed,Sshubam Verma,Mitesh M. Khapra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Vimarsha是一个覆盖印度全部22种法定语言、时长100小时的ASR评测基准，通过现场多样录音与真实困难音频，结合多有效转写变体框架，揭示现有评测的双重偏差并重排SOTA模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有印度语言ASR评测基准存在两类系统性偏差：一是基于干净受控音频导致分数过于乐观，二是转写标准过于严苛、惩罚合理的语言变体导致分数过于悲观。论文旨在同时纠正这两种偏差。

Method: 构建Vimarsha基准，包含100小时、覆盖全部22种印度法定语言的数据。数据由人口统计学上多样化的现场录制音频，以及为声学难度筛选的野外真实音频组成；并提出变体格（lattice of variations）框架，为每句话编码多种有效转写。

Result: 对10个当前最先进ASR模型进行评测，发现在真实条件下模型排名出现显著变化，并存在地理与人口统计上的性能差异，以及在说话语速和声学环境方面呈现系统性失效模式。

Conclusion: Vimarsha通过更真实的音频与更包容的转写标准，提供了更可靠的印度语言ASR评测，暴露了现有基准掩盖的模型排名变动、群体性能差距与系统性失效问题。

Abstract: Evaluation benchmarks for Indian language automatic speech recognition (ASR) suffer from two systematic biases: optimistic scores from clean, controlled audio conditions, and pessimistic scores from overly rigid transcription standards that penalize valid linguistic variations. We introduce Vimarsha, a 100-hour benchmark spanning all 22 scheduled Indian languages, designed to address both distortions. Vimarsha combines demographically diverse on-field recordings with carefully mined in-the-wild audio selected for acoustic difficulty, alongside a lattice of variations framework that encodes multiple valid transcriptions per utterance. Evaluations of 10 state-of-the-art ASR models reveal substantial shifts in model rankings under realistic conditions, geographic and demographic performance disparities, and systematic failure modes across speaking rates and acoustic environments.

</details>


### [370] [From Articles to Publishers: Aggregating Language Model Predictions for News Source Reliability Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24219)
*John Bianchi,Manuel Pratelli,Fabio Pinelli,Marinella Petrocchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将新闻来源可靠性推断建模为来源级别的预测问题，提出两阶段框架：先用基于Transformer的语言模型估计单篇文章可靠性，再聚合文章级预测以推断未见出版商的可靠性；在严格的出版商不重叠评估协议下，聚合显著提升了稳健性与性能（准确率约从0.60提升至0.69）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统新闻出版商可靠性评估依赖专家机构从编辑规范、透明度与事实标准等来源层面进行评判，而现有计算方法多将问题转化为文章级别的分类任务，与真实的来源级评估目标存在错位，因此需要研究来源级别的可靠性推断。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段使用基于Transformer的语言模型估计单篇文章的可靠性；第二阶段将文章级预测聚合，推断此前未见出版商的可靠性。为贴近真实部署条件，采用严格的出版商不重叠评估协议，确保训练集与测试集中不出现同一出版商。实验使用439家英语出版商、19,476篇政治新闻文章，标注为NewsGuard可靠性评分。

Result: 聚合方法显著提升了稳健性与性能，准确率从文章级别的约0.60提升至出版商级别的0.69。此外，分析发现预测误差在不同政治倾向之间存在差异，政治倾向与误分类模式之间存在统计显著关联。

Conclusion: 出版商可靠性可以仅从聚合的文本信号中推断出来，这为可扩展的、基于内容的自动化新闻来源评估提供了支持。

Abstract: Traditionally, the reliability of news publishers is assessed by expert organisations that evaluate editorial practices, transparency and factual standards at source. When this process is translated into a computational approach, the problem is often formulated at the level of individual articles, with models being trained on a set of pre-labelled articles and their performance being evaluated in a test phase. In this work, we investigate news source reliability inference as a source-level prediction problem. We propose a two-stage framework in which transformer-based language models first estimate the reliability of individual articles and subsequently aggregate article-level predictions to infer the reliability of previously unseen publishers. To approximate realistic deployment conditions, we enforce a strict publisher-disjoint evaluation protocol, ensuring that no publisher appears in both training and test sets. Experiments on 19,476 political news articles from 439 English-language publishers labeled with NewsGuard reliability ratings show that aggregation substantially improves robustness and performance, increasing accuracy from approximately 0.60 at the article level to 0.69 at the publisher level. Finally, we analyze how prediction errors vary across political orientations, revealing statistically significant associations between political leaning and misclassification patterns. Overall, our findings show that publisher reliability can be inferred from aggregated textual signals alone, supporting scalable and content-based approaches to automated news source assessment.

</details>


### [371] [Memory vs. Context? Influential Factors of Factual Recall in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24238)
*Guilhem Fouilhé,Nicholas Asher,Philippe Muller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Yu等人(2023)关于语言模型如何在记忆知识与上下文冲突信息之间取舍的研究进行了复现与压力测试，在31个模型及5种新知识关系类型上验证了大部分原始结论，同时发现若干结论不能稳健泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yu等人(2023)刻画了语言模型如何在记忆知识与矛盾的上下文陈述之间进行仲裁，但其结论是否稳健、是否可推广到其他模型与提示尚不明确，因此需要对原始工作进行复现和压力测试。

Method: 在Pythia、GPT-2、Qwen3和Ministral四个模型家族的31个模型（含基座与后训练变体）上复现世界首都实验，并将评估扩展到ParaConflict数据集中另外五种知识关系类型。

Result: 实证确认了大部分原始发现：更大的模型和更高频的实体倾向于偏好记忆答案，且存在显著的家族层面差异。但多项结论无法干净地泛化：实体频率效应在Qwen3-14B和32B上消失；后训练对记忆—上下文权衡的影响在不同家族间不一致；仅改变问题措辞就能使模型对记忆知识的依赖变化高达80个百分点；语义无关的文本也能模拟出连贯的支持性上下文。

Conclusion: 本文厘清了Yu等人主张成立的边界条件及其向其他提示的泛化程度。

Abstract: We reproduce and stress-test the work of Yu et al. (2023), who characterize how language models (LMs) arbitrate between memorized knowledge and contradictory in-context statements. We replicate their world-capitals experiments on 31 models spanning Pythia, GPT-2, Qwen3, and Ministral families, including base and post-trained variants, and extend evaluations to five additional knowledge relation types from the ParaConflict dataset. We empirically confirm most of their original findings: larger models and higher-frequency entities tend to favor memorized answers, with substantial family-level variance. However, several conclusions do not generalize cleanly: entity-frequency effects disappear on Qwen3-14B and 32B; post-training shifts the memory-context trade-off inconsistently across families; question phrasing alone can change a model's reliance on memorized knowledge by up to 80 percentage points; and semantically unrelated prose can mimic coherent supporting context. Our results clarify where Yu et al.'s claims hold and to what extent they generalize to other prompts.

</details>


### [372] [Taramandal-GPT: Enhancing Astrodynamics Problem-Solving with Knowledge Retrieval and Structured Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24246)
*Akhil Sharma,Jatin Gupta,Ali Imam Abidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Taramandal-GPT，一个基于Qwen3-8b的天体动力学领域专用框架，结合RAG与回退机制，并在新构建的APBench基准上取得与主流模型相当的性能，尤其擅长推理密集型任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自然语言理解上进步显著，但在天文学与天体动力学等专业领域效果受限，主要受制于多步推理、符号运算及领域术语等挑战，因此需要面向特定领域进行适配与增强。

Method: 以Qwen3-8b为骨干模型，构建领域适配框架Taramandal-GPT，引入检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，并设计回退机制以提升上下文精确性；同时构建APBench基准，包含299道涵盖基础到高级空间科学的问题。

Result: 采用数值裕度评分与语义相似度评估的双重评价方法，Taramandal-GPT在性能上与当前最先进的开源及闭源模型具有竞争力，在思考密集型任务上表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了专用大语言模型在追求准确性与可解释性的领域中的价值，将Taramandal-GPT定位为面向天体物理、航天器工程与太空探索的可靠AI助手的重要一步。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable progress in natural language understanding, yet their effectiveness in specialized fields like astronomy and astrodynamics remains limited due to challenges in multi-step reasoning, symbolic manipulation, and domain-specific terminology. To address this, we present Taramandal-GPT (Constellation-GPT), a domain-adapted framework built on the Qwen3-8b backbone, enhanced with a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline and a fallback mechanism for improved contextual precision. We evaluate it on the Astrodynamics Problems Benchmark (APBench), a dataset of 299 questions covering foundational to advanced levels of space science. Using a dual evaluation method - numeric margin-based scoring and semantic similarity assessment - Taramandal-GPT achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art open- and closed-source models, with notable strength in thinking-intensive tasks. These results highlight the value of specialized LLMs for domains demanding accuracy and interpretability, positioning Taramandal-GPT as a step toward reliable Artificial Intelligence (AI) assistants for astrophysics, spacecraft engineering, and space exploration.

</details>


### [373] [Canonical Procedural Actions: An Auditable Annotation Protocol for Tool-Use Agent Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24264)
*Songqi Li,Dongqing Li,Zheqiao Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"规范程序动作"(CPAs)标注协议，用于记录工具使用智能体轨迹中的程序功能、事件锚点与证据联系，并通过零售案例研究构建24条目编码手册，测量其结构可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用智能体的轨迹虽能识别消息与API调用，但程序性分析仍缺乏明确的动作单元及其可检验的证据链接，需要一种显式、可审计的标注工具。

Method: 设计CPAs标注协议，记录程序功能、首个智能体事件锚点、实现该功能的事件及独立上下文证据；通过开放归纳、记录整合与连续应用审计，构建版本化24条目编码手册；由两个隔离的LLM上下文对32条与开发集不相交的轨迹进行标注，并做回顾性对照。

Result: 两次标注分别产生499和491个事件，锚点标签重叠度A=0.982，要求上下文事件引用完全一致时降至0.798；折叠所有标签后重叠升至0.986，简单端点规则对工具锚定部分达0.997；助手消息动作重叠0.971（单标签最低0.816）；冻结编码手册应用于244条轨迹产生4,058条记录，含八项诊断结果。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于提供一个显式、可审计的标注工具及其构建与测量局限的案例研究；这些是结构可重复性指标而非语义准确性，人类参照效度与下游效用仍待验证。

Abstract: Tool-use agent traces identify messages and API calls, but procedural analyses also need explicit units of action and inspectable links to their evidence. We present Canonical Procedural Actions (CPAs), an annotation protocol that records a procedural function, its first agent-event anchor, the agent events that realize it, and separate contextual evidence. Multiple actions may share a message anchor without an inferred within-message order. A retail case study produces a versioned 24-entry codebook through open induction, recorded consolidation, and successive application audits. Two isolated LLM contexts annotate 32 trajectories disjoint from development at the trajectory level, producing 499 and 491 occurrences with anchor-label overlap A=0.982. Requiring identical context-event references reduces overlap to 0.798. These are structural repeatability measures, not semantic accuracy: 16 of 26 task IDs also occur in development, and historical tool payloads were truncated to 110 characters. Retrospective controls show that collapsing all labels raises overlap to 0.986, while simple endpoint rules reproduce the tool-anchored portion with 0.997 overlap. Assistant-message actions have 0.971 overlap, with a per-label minimum of 0.816. Applying the frozen codebook to 244 further trajectories yields 4,058 records, including eight diagnostic outcomes. The contribution is an explicit, auditable annotation instrument and a case study of its construction and measurement limits; human-reference validity and downstream utility remain to be established.

</details>


### [374] [Structure Before Sampling: Community-Aware Core-Set Selection for Data-Efficient Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24275)
*Mizbaul Haque Maruf,Muhammad Nur Yanhaona*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用音位组图建模语音语料，并设计Community Representative选择器进行核心集选择，在孟加拉语和英语上均优于随机与熵基方法，且20%核心集训练的TTS模型CER更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTS语料录制成本高，而许多话语几乎不提供新的语音信息。核心集选择可在固定音频时长预算下挑选小规模训练子集以降低成本，因此需要一种能高效保留语音信息（尤其稀有音素）的选择方法。

Method: 将语料表示为音位组图，把每句话与其音素最相似的话语相连；先验证该图是否具有结构。随后提出Community Representative选择器：在图的各社区间采样，并在每个社区内分散选择，从稀有音素丰富的话语开始。

Result: 在孟加拉语和英语语料中，图的聚类系数分别为同规模随机图的199倍和56倍，模块度超过度保持随机图的两倍以上。各预算与两种语言下，该方法覆盖的稀有音素二元组均多于随机和熵基选择，且在留出话语上保持优势。用其20%核心集训练的TTS模型CER显著低于等时长随机或熵基子集。相同训练轮数下，孟加拉语核心集模型以4.5倍更少训练时间超过全语料训练（CER 3.93% vs. 4.47%）。

Conclusion: 音位组图具有显著社区结构，利用该结构的Community Representative核心集选择可更高效地覆盖稀有音素信息，在降低录制与训练成本的同时提升或保持TTS性能。

Abstract: Text-to-speech (TTS) corpora are costly to record, yet many utterances add little new phonetic information. Core-set selection reduces this cost by choosing a small training subset under a fixed audio-duration budget. We represent a corpus as a phonotactic graph that links each utterance to its most phonemically similar ones, and we first test whether this graph has structure. In Bangla and English corpora, its clustering is 199 and 56 times that of a size-matched random graph, and its modularity is more than twice that of a degree-preserving random graph. We then propose Community Representative, a selector that samples across graph communities and spreads its choices within each one, starting from utterances rich in rare phonemes. At every budget and in both languages, it covers more rare phoneme bigrams than random and entropy-based selection, and this lead holds on held-out utterances. TTS models trained on its 20% core-sets have a significantly lower character error rate (CER) than models trained on equal-duration random or entropy-based subsets in both languages. When all models train for the same number of epochs, the Bangla core-set model also outperforms full-corpus training (3.93% vs. 4.47% CER) with 4.5x less training time.

</details>


### [375] [Mitigating Entity Type Confusion in Cross-Domain NER via Multidimensional Quantification and Reasoning Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24357)
*Jingyu Wang,Shijie Wu,Fusheng Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对跨域命名实体识别中实体类型混淆问题，提出多维混淆量化模型（MCQM）与渐进式双向推理链（PBRC），在CrossNER数据集所有域上取得新的SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域命名实体识别（CD-NER）旨在将源域知识迁移至目标域。现有分解或生成范式虽在实体跨度检测上表现优异，但在实体类型分类时严重存在实体类型混淆问题，即模型倾向于将某类实体错误地归为另一相似但错误的类型，制约了模型性能与泛化能力。

Method: 首先提出多维混淆量化模型（MCQM），从三个维度量化模型在实体类型间的混淆程度：源-目标层次分析、语义相似性分析和显式数据评估。其次提出渐进式双向推理链（PBRC），利用MCQM得到的源-目标层次与混淆分析，引导大语言模型生成两阶段推理信息；该推理信息用于增强模型知识，从而缓解实体类型混淆并提升泛化性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在CrossNER数据集的所有域上均取得了新的最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 通过量化并利用实体类型混淆信息，结合大语言模型的两阶段推理增强，可有效缓解跨域命名实体识别中的类型混淆问题，显著提升模型的泛化能力与整体性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain Named Entity Recognition (CD-NER) aims to transfer the rich knowledge in the source domain to the target domain. Recent studies adopting decomposition or generation paradigms have achieved significant performance improvements, demonstrating high accuracy in entity span detection. However, during entity type classification, models severely suffer from entity type confusion, the erroneous tendency that models classify entities of one type in the text as another similar but incorrect type. To address this issue, we first propose a Multidimensional Confusion Quantification Model (MCQM) that quantifies a model's confusion extent between entity types from three dimensions: source-target hierarchy analysis, semantic similarity analysis, and explicit data evaluation. Moreover, we propose the Progressive Bidirectional Reasoning Chain (PBRC). PBRC leverages the source-target hierarchy and confusion analysis from the MCQM to prompt the LLM to generate two-stage reasoning information. The two-stage reasoning information is utilized to augment the knowledge of the model, significantly mitigating entity type confusion and improving the model's generalization performance. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves new state-of-the-art results on all domains of the CrossNER dataset.

</details>


### [376] [URA-NER: A Unified Retrieval-Augmented Framework with Retrieval Alignment and Uncertainty Reduction for Low-Resource NER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24372)
*Jingyu Wang,Shijie Wu,Fusheng Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的检索增强框架 URA-NER，通过渐进粒度检索、模型感知表示增强和推理感知知识验证三个组件，缓解低资源命名实体识别中的检索错位与生成不确定性问题，并在多个基准上取得新最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的上下文学习在缓解命名实体识别标注数据不足问题上有潜力，但现有方法存在检索错位和生成不确定性问题，性能高度依赖模型能力。当模型参数规模减小时，少样本场景下的性能明显下降，因此需要改进框架以提升低资源条件下的表现。

Method: 提出统一检索增强框架 URA-NER，包含三个关键组件：1）渐进粒度检索（PGR），两阶段检索机制实现阶段对齐，先根据查询的全局语义检索用于跨度检测的示例，再根据具体实体上下文检索用于类型分类的示例；2）模型感知表示增强（MaRE），利用实体预识别指导表示构建，使查询与示例在大语言模型语义空间和注意力模式中对齐；3）推理感知知识验证（RaKV），构建“生成-检索-验证”的闭环过程，显式化大语言模型的推理路径，据此检索外部知识并重组为与原始推理路径对齐的验证证据，以缓解生成不确定性。

Result: 在多个低资源命名实体识别数据集上进行大量实验，结果表明 URA-NER 显著提升了低资源设置下大语言模型的性能，对较小规模的大语言模型提升尤为明显，并在多个基准上取得新的最优结果。

Conclusion: URA-NER 通过检索与验证的统一设计，有效缓解检索错位和生成不确定性问题，提升了低资源命名实体识别性能，尤其有利于参数规模较小的模型，并在多个基准上达到新最优水平。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) based on large language models (LLMs) has shown promising potential in alleviating performance bottlenecks caused by the limited availability of annotated data in Named Entity Recognition (NER). However, existing methods still face issues of retrieval misalignment and generation uncertainty, making their performance heavily dependent on the LLM's capabilities. As the parameter scale of LLMs decreases, their performance in few-shot settings deteriorates significantly. In this paper, we propose a novel unified retrieval-augmented framework, URA-NER, including three key components: Progressive Granularity Retrieval (PGR), Model-aware Representation Enhancement (MaRE), and Reason-aware Knowledge Verification. PGR is a two-stage retrieval mechanism that achieves stage alignment. It first retrieves demonstrations for span detection based on the query's global semantics, and then for type classification based on the specific entity context, providing fine-grained local information. Moreover, MaRE employs entity pre-recognition to guide the construction of representations, ensuring the query and demonstrations are aligned within the LLM's semantic space and attention pattern. In addition, to mitigate generation uncertainty, we propose RaKV, a closed-loop "generation-retrieval-verification" process. It explicates the LLM's reasoning paths, leverages them for the retrieval of external knowledge, and reorganizes the knowledge into verification evidence aligned with the original reasoning paths. We conduct extensive experiments on multiple low-resource NER datasets. Results demonstrate that URA-NER significantly enhances the performance of LLMs under low-resource settings, with particularly pronounced gains for smaller LLMs, achieving new state-of-the-art results on several benchmarks.

</details>


### [377] [End-to-end Jordanian dialect speech-to-text self-supervised learning framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24410)
*Ali A. Safieh,Ibrahim Abu Alhaol,Rawan Ghnemat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Transformer的端到端框架，专门用于低资源语言（约旦阿拉伯语方言）的语音转文本，通过自监督预训练、噪声学生训练和多种数据增强，词错误率比微调Wav2Vec模型降低5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音转文本引擎在人机交互中不可或缺，但阿拉伯方言及许多低资源语言缺乏标注语音数据。自监督训练和利用噪声数据的自训练被认为是可行的解决方案，因此需要构建能充分利用无标注数据的低资源语音识别框架。

Method: 提出端到端、基于Transformer的模型及面向低资源语言的框架，包含定制的音频到文本处理算法。框架支持多源数据摄取、借助外部资源加速人工标注获得真实标签，并采用噪声学生训练与自监督学习，在预训练和训练后阶段利用无标注数据，结合多种数据增强方式。

Result: 所提出的自训练方法在词错误率上比微调后的Wav2Vec模型降低了5%。同时为研究社区提供了约旦口语数据集。

Conclusion: 该工作为低资源语言处理提供了端到端方法，通过预训练、训练后处理以及注入带噪标注和增强数据，以极少人工干预实现高效语音识别，可推动阿拉伯语语音转文本在问答系统和智能控制等应用中的发展，并为智能机器人增加类人听觉感知能力。

Abstract: Speech-to-text engines are extremely needed nowadays for different applications, representing an essential enabler in human-robot interaction. Still, some languages suffer from the lack of labeled speech data, especially in the Arabic dialects or any low-resource languages. The need for a self-supervised training process and self-training using noisy training is proven to be one of the up-and-coming feasible solutions. This article proposes an end-to-end, transformers-based model with a framework for low-resource languages. In addition, the framework incorporates customized audio-to-text processing algorithms to achieve a highly efficient Jordanian Arabic dialect speech-to-text system. The proposed framework enables ingesting data from many sources, making the ground truth from external sources possible by speeding up the manual annotation process. The framework allows the training process using noisy student training and self-supervised learning to utilize the unlabeled data in both pre- and post-training stages and incorporate multiple types of data augmentation. The proposed self-training approach outperforms the fine-tuned Wav2Vec model by 5% in terms of word error rate reduction. The outcome of this work provides the research community with a Jordanian-spoken data set along with an end-to-end approach to deal with low-resource languages. This is done by utilizing the power of the pretraining, post-training, and injecting noisy labeled and augmented data with minimal human intervention. It enables the development of new applications in the field of Arabic language speech-to-text area like the question-answering systems and intelligent control systems, and it will add human-like perception and hearing sensors to intelligent robots.

</details>


### [378] [LLJ Cards: Best practices for the Use of LLMs as Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24516)
*Khaoula Chehbouni,Melina Medjdoub,Florian Carichon,Golnoosh Farnadi,Jackie Chi Kit Cheung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LLJ Cards 框架，借鉴测量理论、自然语言生成与机器学习的最佳实践，为基于大模型评估者（LLJs）的评估提供规范化、透明且可复现的实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型评估者（LLJs）因性能强、可扩展、成本低而被广泛用于评估任务，但其有效性与可靠性受到质疑。现有工作多集中于偏差缓解技术和提示策略优化，属于技术性修补，未解决更根本的问题：缺乏标准化、透明、可复现的评估实践。

Method: 本文提出 LLJ Cards 框架，将测量理论、自然语言生成与机器学习文献中的最佳实践综合为面向 LLJ 评估的实用指南，为自动化评估的设计与报告提供结构化框架。

Result: 论文通过框架整合形成了面向 LLJ 评估的实践准则，强调将严谨的评估原则嵌入自动化评估的设计与报告流程，但摘要未提供具体实验结果或量化验证。

Conclusion: LLJs 是通向可扩展评估的有前景路径，但其有效使用必须以严谨的评估原则为基础，以确保有效性、可靠性与可复现性；LLJ Cards 通过结构化框架满足这一需求。

Abstract: In recent years, large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a popular alternative for evaluation. Often referred to as LLMs as judges (LLJs), these systems have been widely adopted by researchers and practitioners across a broad range of measurement tasks, driven by their strong performance, scalability, and cost-effectiveness relative to human judgment. However, a growing body of work has shown that the use of LLJs raise concerns about their validity and reliability as evaluators. Existing efforts to address these challenges have largely focused on developing bias-mitigation techniques and refining prompting strategies. While these approaches represent an important step forward, they primarily offer technical fixes and leave a more fundamental challenge unaddressed: the lack of standardized, transparent, and reproducible evaluation practices. In this paper, we introduce LLJ Cards, a framework that synthesizes best practices from measurement theory, natural language generation, and machine learning literature into practical guidelines for LLJ-based evaluations. While LLJs offer a promising path toward scalable evaluation, their effective use requires grounding in rigorous evaluation principles to ensure validity, reliability, and reproducibility. LLJ Cards addresses this need by providing a structured framework for applying these principles in the design and reporting of automated evaluations.

</details>


### [379] [QLoRA Fine-Tuning of Ministral LLM for Sequence-to-Function Protein Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24538)
*Demian Pavlyshenko,Bohdan Pavlyshenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将新测序蛋白质的功能注释建模为序列到文本的生成任务，用QLoRA微调30亿参数的Ministral 3基座模型，并通过"LLM作为专家"协议评估，认为紧凑型LLM能为相当一部分蛋白质生成具有生物学价值的注释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共数据库中蛋白质序列的增长速度远超人工注释能力，功能注释成为分子生物学的瓶颈；现有计算方法多把注释视为固定本体上的多标签分类，预测被限制在预定义标签集内。

Method: 将蛋白质注释视为序列到文本的生成问题，在"序列-注释"配对数据上用QLoRA（4比特NF4量化结合低秩适配器）微调30亿参数的Ministral 3基座模型；采用"LLM作为专家"协议评估，即用被提示为资深分子生物学策展人的GPT模型对生物体识别作二分类打分，并评估功能注释质量。

Result: 经QLoRA微调的紧凑型LLM能够为相当一部分蛋白质生成具备真实生物学价值的策展人风格注释。

Conclusion: 该方法在部分蛋白质上具备实用潜力，但要达到实际可用的可靠性，仍需在数据质量、模型规模扩展和证据支撑（evidence grounding）等方向继续改进。

Abstract: Functional annotation of newly sequenced proteins remains a bottleneck in molecular biology: the number of sequences in public repositories grows far faster than the capacity for manual curation. Most computational approaches consider annotation as multi-label classification over a fixed ontology, which constrains predictions to a predefined label set. In this work we study the the protein annotation as a sequence-to-text generation problem. We fine-tune the 3B-parameter Ministral 3 base model with QLoRA (4-bit NF4 quantization with low-rank adapters) on sequence annotation pairs. We assess predictions with an LLM-as-expert protocol: a GPT model prompted as a senior molecular-biology curator scores organism identification as binary and function annotation quality. We conclude that QLoRA-fine-tuned compact LLMs can generate curator-style annotations with genuine biological value for a substantial subset of proteins. We also discuss future directions in data quality, model scaling, and evidence grounding that are needed to make the approach sufficiently reliable for practical use.

</details>


### [380] [Toward a Unified Mathematics of Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24554)
*Chen Shani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"基于操作的视角"，通过十三个概念操作来评估十种概念表征框架，并以分类任务实证验证：数学形式的选择决定了理论能解释什么，因此呼吁融合内容、关系与过程的混合形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念被普遍视为智能行为的基本单元，但认知科学、心理学与人工智能之间缺乏共享的数学语言。现有系统用向量、分布、符号、图等不同结构表示概念，这些形式化通常被当作彼此竞争的方案，而非对同一问题的不同解答。作者希望建立一个共同标准来比较这些框架。

Method: 提出操作导向的评估框架：识别出跨认知、心理学与AI反复出现的十三个操作（如相似性、组合、泛化、接地等）；分析十种框架，将其归纳为对概念的三类承诺——自足内容、关系结构、演化过程，并考察各框架自然支持哪些操作。随后以"分类"为案例进行实证检验，在同一批项目上操作化九种理论，并与人类判断进行比较（必要说明：摘要未披露具体数据集、被试规模及统计指标等细节）。

Result: 十种框架体现出不同的概念承诺，而这些承诺决定了它们天然支持的操作集合：基于向量的模型擅长度量相似性与泛化，却难以实现显式组合；符号模型支持组合，但泛化能力弱。所考察的框架中，没有任何单一框架能在不加以扩展的情况下自然支持全部操作。在分类案例中，九种理论虽针对同一概念问题，却产生不同的操作程序与结果。

Conclusion: 数学承诺塑造了理论能够解释的范围，形式化选择本身即具有解释性后果。作者呼吁发展混合形式化，将内容、关系与过程视为同等基本，而非相互替代。

Abstract: Concepts are commonly defined as abstract, compact representations of knowledge and treated as basic units of intelligent behavior. Yet, cognition, psychology, and AI lack a shared mathematical language for them. Modern systems represent concepts as vectors, distributions, symbols, graphs, and other structures, but these formalisms are typically treated as competing rather than as solutions to a common problem. We propose an operation-based view that evaluates mathematical frameworks by the conceptual operations they support, identifying thirteen operations (including similarity, composition, generalization, and grounding) that recur across cognition, psychology, and AI. We show that ten frameworks embody distinct commitments to concepts as self-contained content, relational structure, or evolving process, and that these commitments determine which operations each supports naturally. For example, vector-based models facilitate graded similarity and generalization but struggle with explicit composition, whereas symbolic models support composition but offer but generalize poorly. No single framework we examined naturally supports all operations without extension. We test this account empirically using categorization as a case study, operationalizing nine theories on the same items against human judgments. Despite addressing the same conceptual question, the theories produce different procedures and results, demonstrating that mathematical commitment shapes what a theory can explain. We call for hybrid formalisms that treat content, relation, and process as jointly primary.

</details>


### [381] [Evaluating Decision Models for Text Annotation in Computational Social Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24574)
*Hazem Ibrahim,Yasir Zaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在18个计算社会科学分类任务上评测了决策模型（decision models），发现其准确率不及最优LLM（中位差距11.6个macro-F1点），但成本低约44倍，且置信度校准优于多数LLM，可作为标注流水线的低成本前置环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算社会科学越来越依赖大语言模型进行文本标注，标注效度取决于模型生成的标签。决策模型是面向分类问答的新型模型，以更低的推理价格输出选择、标签概率分布和置信分数，但其在社会科学构念上的准确性与置信度可信度尚不明确。

Method: 沿用Ziems等人（2024）的评测框架，在18个计算社会科学分类任务（7,977个项目）上，以相同的零样本协议，将一个商用决策模型及两个开源权重同类模型，与19个前沿及开源权重语言模型进行对比。

Result: 决策模型在15个可比较任务中的14个上落后于各任务最优LLM，macro-F1中位差距11.6分，但实测成本中位低44倍。其置信度校准优于19个LLM中的16个，但仍有3个前沿模型校准误差更低（0.157对比0.066）。置信度高于0.9的项目通常标注准确（中位准确率0.815），但在同伴支持对话共情任务上，模型高置信却接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 决策模型适合作为标注流水线的第一步：将低置信项目路由给LLM，可在LLM单独使用成本的1/4至1/2下达到或超过其效果。

Abstract: Computational social science increasingly relies on large language models for text annotation, and the validity of published findings now rests on the labels generated by such models. Decision models, a new model class built for categorical question answering, answer typed questions with a choice, a probability distribution over the label set, and a confidence score rather than free text, at a small fraction of frontier inference prices. Whether their answers are accurate, and whether that stated confidence can be trusted on social science constructs, are unknown. Here, we mirror the evaluation of Ziems et al. (2024) on 18 computational social science classification tasks (7,977 items), comparing the first commercial decision model and two open-weight counterparts against 19 frontier and open-weight language models under the same zero-shot protocol. The decision model trails the per-task best LLM on 14 of 15 evaluation tasks, with a median deficit of 11.6 macro-F1 points, at a median 44 times lower measured cost. Its confidence is better calibrated than the verbalized confidence of 16 of the 19 LLMs, yet three frontier models show lower median calibration error (0.157 against 0.066). While items above 0.9 confidence are typically labeled accurately (median accuracy 0.815), on one task, empathy in peer-support dialogues, the model reports high confidence while performing near chance. Nonetheless, our results suggest that decision models are useful as a first step in the annotation pipeline: routing low-confidence items to an LLM matches or exceeds the LLM alone at a quarter to half of its cost.

</details>


### [382] [Written as a Record, Read as an Address: What a Forward Pass Leaves in an Operation's KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24635)
*Lingfeng Wu,Behzad Shomali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语言模型在执行操作指令时，会在KV缓存的操作token位置写入局部化的、可因果恢复的记录，这些记录既支持路由也支持直接载荷访问，但写这些记录的模型主要读取其中的地址而非值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于实体追踪的工作确立了模型在查询时解析绑定，而非存储为显式隐状态，但没有回答模型在操作跨度上究竟写了什么、以及这些信息如何被访问。本文旨在探究操作token在KV缓存中留下的记录内容及其访问机制。

Method: 将前向传播拆分为冻结的写入者和读取者：写入者的缓存无梯度重算，读取者仅看到指令和操作token（隐藏所有状态描述），并单独训练。在合成盒子任务上训练读取者，通过操作跨度移植测试因果关系，并在Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B以及ToMi、GSM8K上验证。

Result: 基线读取者对查询绑定的恢复率≤0.06，训练后达0.75–1.00，可恢复性随操作的读写足迹变化。发现两种访问模式：操作跨度移植能因果性地重定向所读取的可见状态（路由记录）；隔离训练保留路由并增加对载荷的直接访问，位于中层窄带（Llama-3.1-8B第12–15层，Mistral-7B第14–17层）的单个操作数名称token上。该配方可扩展到ToMi和GSM8K，但受训练覆盖限制并牺牲开卷准确率。

Conclusion: 操作token留下了局部化、可因果恢复的记录，同时支持路由和直接载荷访问，但写入这些记录的模型主要读取其中的地址而非值。

Abstract: When a language model reads an operation such as "Swap the contents of Box F and Box B", its forward pass writes keys and values for those tokens into the KV cache. Prior work on entity tracking establishes what models use: bindings are resolved at query time rather than stored as explicit latent state. We ask what they write at the operation span and how it is accessed. We split a forward pass into a frozen writer and a reader: the writer's cache is recomputed without gradients, while the reader sees only the instruction and operation tokens, with all state descriptions hidden, and is trained in isolation. Anything the reader recovers was therefore already present in the unmodified cache. On a synthetic boxes task, a base reader recovers $\leq 0.06$ of queried bindings against $0.75$--$1.00$ after training, and recoverability tracks the operation's read/write footprint. We find two modes of access. Across Llama-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B, operation-span transplants causally redirect which visible state is read even when the two worlds hold identical values, revealing a routing record. Isolation training preserves routing and adds direct access to the payload, the value the operation read, from the single operand-name token in a narrow mid-depth band (layers 12--15 of 32 in Llama-3.1-8B, 14--17 in Mistral-7B) --- the same site that holds the routing record. The same recipe extends to further operations, ToMi and GSM8K, but is bounded by training coverage and costs open-book accuracy. Operation tokens thus leave localized, causally recoverable records that support both routing and direct payload access, though the model that writes them reads mainly the address they carry and not the value.

</details>


### [383] [Assessing Readability with LLMs: The Role of Reasoning and Few-Shot Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24650)
*Raphaël Thieffry,Matej Martinc*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了多种开源大语言模型在多语言（英语与低资源斯洛文尼亚语）可读性评估中的表现，重点考察提示策略（CoT、少样本）的影响，并证明开箱即用的LLM可作为稳健的跨语言可读性评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可读性公式难以跨体裁、跨语言泛化；有监督机器学习模型依赖稀缺且领域特定的标注语料，在低资源语言上适用性受限。LLM虽具可扩展、多语言、免任务训练的优势，但其先进提示策略对可读性评估性能的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对多种开源LLM进行系统性基准测试，用于多语言可读性评估，任务为预测教育框架所要求的离散可读性等级。在英语之外引入低资源语言斯洛文尼亚语。考察显式推理的作用（CoT提示与推理导向模型对比直接作答），并研究少样本上下文学习（每类别1个标注样本 vs 零样本及更多样本）。将上述方法与传统无监督指标及当前最优有监督基线进行对比。

Result: CoT提示与推理导向模型相比直接作答有显著提升；每类别仅提供1个标注样本（1-shot）即可较零样本大幅提升预测质量，而增加更多示例收益递减；通过与无监督指标和有监督基线的全面比较，验证了LLM作为跨语言可读性评估器的稳健性与可行性。

Conclusion: 开箱即用的LLM可作为稳健、跨语言的可读性评估器，在低资源语言场景下依然有效；显式推理与最少量的少样本示例是提升性能的关键，为可读性评估提供了无需任务特定训练的可扩展替代方案。

Abstract: Readability assessment is essential for tailoring texts to intended audiences across educational, healthcare, and information retrieval domains. However, traditional readability formulas struggle to generalize across genres and languages, while supervised machine learning models rely on scarce, domain-specific annotated corpora, limiting their applicability--particularly for less-resourced languages. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a highly scalable, multilingual alternative that requires no task-specific training, yet the impact of advanced prompting strategies on their performance remains underexplored.
